At the END of every task (after code, lint and tests pass) update every affected section — files, functions, models, env vars, errors, tests, ticket progress — and the `Last updated` line. Accuracy and completeness over length: never shorten or drop existing info. No code snippets, no secrets (env var names only, never values).

**API contract hardening (2026-11-08):** three tasks, done with `docs/openapi.yaml` as the client contract. (1) **Rate limiters:** every route now has one (new `staffLimiter`, `coachLimiter`, `coachReadLimiter`, `profileLimiter`, `publicLimiter`); `publicLimiter` also covers `/health/ready`, `/api-docs`, the 404 fallback and every 405; only `GET /health` is unlimited; guarded by `routes.rate-limit-coverage.test.js`. (2) **One list shape:** `?page&limit` (default 20, max 100) and `{ data, pagination: { page, limit, total, totalPages } }` on plans, classes, sessions, coaches, cafe items, inventory and orders; `GET /plans` adds a top-level `counts`; `meta`, `pageSize`, `buildMeta`, `buildSizeField` and the per-feature paging constants are gone. (3) **Status codes and messages:** validation is 400 everywhere (was 422 for plans, memberships, classes, slots, sessions and cafe), including malformed path ids (new `idParam.validation.js`; was 404 for plans and classes) and the cafe image-token MIME check; a private session or a class the caller may not see is 404 (was 403); P2025 races on cafe category/item writes are 404 (was 500, `orNotFound`); an unmapped P2002 is a generic 409 (was "User already exists"); the register 409 and the 413 carry a `code`; wrong messages fixed ("Item ID", "Limit must be a whole number between 1 and 100", `CLASS_INACTIVE`); no `Location` header on `POST /memberships` and `POST /cafe/categories` (no GET by id); every literal message moved to `messages.js`; plan error codes are UPPER_SNAKE; 405 + `Allow` for the auth, staff, coach, uploads and health routers (was 404); a missing body is `field: "body"`; the 404 message no longer echoes the URL. `docs/openapi.yaml` was rewritten against the routers and live responses: 66 operations plus a 405 stub for every path, the 12 operations that were missing (memberships GET/POST, slots x4, sessions x6) and `/api-docs`, 429 on every limited operation, one `ValidationError`, `PlanDetail` / `ClassResponse` fields that were undocumented, three broken `$ref`s fixed (`ErrorResponse`, `NotFound`, `Conflict`). **Behaviour changes to know about (no clients existed yet):** 422 -> 400, `pageSize` -> `limit`, `meta` -> `pagination` + `counts`, class and session default page size 100 -> 20, plan error codes uppercased, 403 -> 404 for hidden sessions and slot lists, 404 -> 405 for wrong methods, 404 -> 400 for malformed ids on plans and classes. **Not changed, still open:** for MEMBER and COACH the `GET /sessions` role filter runs after paging (`pagination.total` is the visible count of the page); the security findings of the 2026-11-07 audit that were out of scope (4-digit OTP with IP-only limits, `Cache-Control`, `mustChangePassword`, public `/api-docs` in production).

**SLAPP-79/82 Cafe order queue, phase 1 (2026-11-06):** read-only order endpoints. (1) Migration `20261106120000_add_cafe_orders` (+ manual `down.sql`): `cafe_orders` (unique `orderNumber`, `status` PREPARING/READY/SERVED, `createdById` NOT NULL FK to `User` ON DELETE RESTRICT, nullable `customerName`, `totalAed`, `paymentStatus` enum with the single value NOT_TRACKED) and `cafe_order_items` (snapshot `itemName`, `unitPriceAed`, `quantity`; no FK to `cafe_items`; a trigger rejects updates to snapshot columns). (2) `GET /api/v1/cafe/orders`, `GET /orders/summary`, `GET /orders/:orderId` for OWNER/ADMIN/COACH (MEMBER 403). Visibility lives only in `src/services/cafeOrder.access.js#buildOrderScope`: OWNER/ADMIN see all, everyone else only `createdById = req.user.id`; out-of-scope detail is 404. Strict query validation on all 3 routes: unknown query params (such as `source`/`userId`) are 400; the detail route accepts no query params. This differs on purpose from the older cafe validators, which strip unknown keys (see §3 "Cafe Orders feature"). Dates are facility-local (Asia/Dubai), inclusive. Summary has no revenue. (3) Test cleanup helpers (`testData.helper`, `sweepTestData`) now delete a user's cafe orders before the user. Parity audit is now 17 endpoints. Order creation is not implemented. The migration is not applied to Neon (verified only on a local throwaway Postgres 16).

**SLAPP-79/82 Cafe order status change, phase 2 (2026-11-06):** `PATCH /api/v1/cafe/orders/:orderId/status`, OWNER/ADMIN only (COACH and MEMBER 403), `cafeLimiter`. Body exactly `{ status }` (lowercase `preparing` | `ready` | `served`; unknown keys 400 "Unknown field"; no query params). Transition map `ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS` (`src/constants/cafeOrder.js`): preparing -> ready -> served, one step, nothing after served, no cancel; anything else is 409 `INVALID_ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITION` with `details { from, to, allowed }`. One conditional `updateMany({ where: { id, status: <read status> } })` in a `$transaction` with the `CAFE_ORDER_STATUS_CHANGED` audit row (new `AuditAction` value, migration `20261106130000_add_cafe_order_status_audit_action`); a lost race re-reads and answers 404 (gone) or 409 (changed). Only `status` and `updatedAt` change; `paymentStatus` stays `not_tracked`. Response = the `GET /orders/:orderId` detail shape. Migration not applied to Neon.

**Catalogue delivery (2026-11-02):** SLAPP-79/80 (Cafe Catalogue & Glofox Seed). (1) Endpoints under `/api/v1/cafe`: Categories CRUD (`GET /categories` with `itemCount`, `POST /categories`, `PATCH /categories/:categoryId`, `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` with 409 `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS` guard); Items CRUD (`GET /items` with pagination, search, category filter, member public isolation; `POST /items`; `GET /items/:itemId`; `PATCH /items/:itemId`; `PATCH /items/:itemId/availability`; `PATCH /items/:itemId/visibility`; `POST /uploads/image/token`). Items are never hard-deleted (`DELETE` returns 405). Reads allowed for OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER; writes allowed for OWNER, ADMIN only. Rate limited via `cafeReadLimiter` on reads and `cafeLimiter` on writes/uploads. (2) Sold-out business logic: tracked items (`isTracked=true`) require `stockQuantity` and `unitCost`, auto-set `sold_out` when stock reaches 0, manual setting to `available` with 0 stock returns 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`; untracked items (made to order: Coffee, Protein Shakes, Protein Coolers) force stock and unitCost to null with manual availability; sold-out items remain visible to members. (3) Glofox seed: `prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js` reads sheet "Store" from `docs/Spirit_Level_Glofox_Business_Data.xlsx` (61 items, 12 categories, 18 untracked, 23 tracked sold out, 20 tracked available, 7 staff_only); supports `--dry-run` and `--force` (default skips items that already exist by `externalRef`; `--force` overwrites them). (4) Data model & Migration: `20261102130000_add_cafe_catalogue` + reversible `down.sql`: creates `cafe_categories` and `cafe_items` tables with CHECK constraints, foreign key `onDelete: Restrict`, and lowercase API / uppercase DB enum mapping. (5) Web/mobile parity: standard envelopes, Bearer JWT, no Origin-dependent behavior. (6) OpenAPI: all 11 cafe operations (plus their 405 handlers) are documented in `docs/openapi.yaml` under the `Cafe` tag. (7) Tests: see section 24 for the final cafe suite counts.

## 1. Stack & Conventions

Node ≥ 20 / Express 5, CommonJS (`require`/`module.exports`), plain JavaScript, function-based modules, async/await throughout. **No TypeScript remains** (verified: no `.ts`/`.d.ts` files, no `tsconfig`, no `ts-node` or `@types/*` in `package.json`).
Prisma 6 + PostgreSQL (Neon). Zod v4 for runtime validation. bcrypt for passwords. pino + pino-http for structured logging.
Runtime deps (`package.json`): `@prisma/client`, `@vercel/blob`, `bcrypt`, `cors`, `dotenv`, `express`, `express-rate-limit`, `helmet`, `jsonwebtoken`, `node-cron`, `nodemailer`, `pino`, `pino-http`, `swagger-ui-express`, `yaml`, `zod`. Dev deps: `jest` 30, `supertest`, `eslint` 10 (flat config) + `@eslint/js` + `eslint-config-prettier` + `globals`, `prettier`, `nodemon`, `pino-pretty`, `prisma`, `exceljs` (seed only). `multer` is **not** installed (removed after the client-upload refactor). Node `crypto` is used for UUIDs, refresh tokens, temp passwords and SHA-256 hashing.
npm scripts: `test` (`jest --runInBand`), `dev` (`nodemon src/server.js`), `start` (`node src/server.js`), `lint` (`eslint .`), `lint:fix`, `format` (`prettier --write .`), `prisma:generate`, `prisma:migrate` (`prisma migrate dev`), `prisma:seed` (`node prisma/seed.js`, owner seed), `seed:membership-plans` (`node prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js`), `seed:classes` (`node prisma/seeds/classes.seed.js`), `seed:cafe` (`node prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js`, flags `--dry-run` / `--force`). Windows note: commands run with `npx.cmd` on the developer machine (on Linux use `npx`).
All env vars are validated at startup via Zod in `src/config/env.js`; the process exits on invalid config.
All user-facing validation/error text lives in `src/constants/messages.js` (one constant per distinct message, no duplicate texts); `auth.validation.js` has no inline message literals.
**Layout (layer-based MVC, since 2026-10-10):** `src/modules/` no longer exists. Files live by layer: `controllers/` (HTTP handlers), `services/` (business logic + `auth.guards`, `login.policies`, `staff.policies`), `repositories/` (Prisma data access + `user.mapper`, `staff.mapper`, `membershipPlan.mapper`), `validators/` (Zod schemas, `*.validation.js`), `routes/` (all route files incl. `auth/staff/uploads.routes.js`). File names are unchanged; the §3 headings below group rows by feature but every path is the real current path.
Patterns in use: Strategy (`login.policies`, `staff.policies`, `coach.access` role rules and `UPDATE_FIELDS_BY_ROLE`, all `Map`-based or frozen tables, deny by default), Chain of Responsibility (`auth.guards`), thin controllers, repositories as the main Prisma callers. Direct Prisma use outside `repositories/` (verified 2026-11-06): `prisma.$transaction` in `staff.service`, `coach.service` and `schedule.service`; `password-reset.service` passes the `prisma` client to `createAuditLog`; `schedule.job` reads `prisma.class.findMany`; `health.routes` runs `prisma.$queryRaw` (`SELECT 1`).
**API contract rules (single standard since 2026-11-08; `docs/openapi.yaml` is the client contract for the Next.js web app and the Kotlin app):**

- **Envelopes:** success `{ success: true, message?, data }`; errors `{ success: false, message, code, errors?, details? }`. `code` is on EVERY error (UPPER_SNAKE_CASE); the 413 and the register 409 used to lack it.
- **Lists** (plans, classes, sessions, coaches, cafe items, inventory, orders): query `?page=1&limit=20` (limit 1-100, default 20, page 1-100000), response `{ success, data, pagination: { page, limit, total, totalPages } }`; `GET /plans` adds a top-level `counts`. No `meta`, no `pageSize`. `GET /memberships` and `GET /classes/:id/slots` return their whole small set without paging.
- **Status codes:** `400 VALIDATION_ERROR` for everything wrong with the request itself (body, query, a path id that is not a UUID, unsupported image type, empty/missing body, malformed JSON); `401`; `403` role not allowed; `404` missing OR not visible to the caller (hidden classes, sessions, orders and staff-only items look like missing ones); `405` + `Allow` for a wrong method on a real path (every router); `409` conflicts; `413` body over 1 MB; `429`. `422` is not used any more.
- **Rate limits:** every route except `GET /health` has a limiter (default 1000 per 15 min): public routes per IP, routes behind login per user + IP after `authenticate` + `authorize`; `publicLimiter` covers `/health/ready`, `/api-docs`, the 404 fallback and all 405 answers. Enforced by `tests/unit/routes.rate-limit-coverage.test.js`.
- **Messages:** every message is a `Messages.*` constant (parametrised ones are functions); no literals in services, validators or middlewares. Unmapped Prisma P2002 / P2025 become generic 409 `RESOURCE_ALREADY_EXISTS` / 404 `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND`; services that know the record throw their own error first (`orNotFound`).
- **Superseded wording:** older ticket paragraphs below were updated in place on 2026-11-08 (422 -> 400, `pageSize`/`meta` -> `limit`/`pagination`, lowercase plan codes -> `PLAN_*`); where one still describes the old behaviour as history it says so.
  See [docs/ONBOARDING.md](./ONBOARDING.md) for full architectural rationale and decisions (`README.md` only contains the title "Backend").

## 2. Request Flow

```
HTTP → helmet → cors (origin allow-list) → clientType (sets req.clientType) → pino-http (logs clientType) → express.json (1mb) → express.urlencoded → route
     → authLimiter (auth routes) → validateBody(schema) → controller → service → repository → Prisma
                                                                        ↓ throw / next(err)
                                                        errorHandler (4-arg Express middleware)
Unmatched routes → publicLimiter → notFound → errorHandler (404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`, fixed message)
Wrong method on a real path → publicLimiter → methodNotAllowed (405 + `Allow`, before authentication) → errorHandler
Body over 1 MB → express.json → errorHandler (413 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`)
```

Login flow detail (`POST /api/v1/auth/login`): see §9 "Implemented login flow".

Staff creation flow (`POST /api/v1/admin/staff`): `authenticate` (Bearer JWT, DB role/status) → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `validateCreateStaff` → `staff.controller.createStaff` → `staff.service.createStaff` → repositories. Rate limited by `staffLimiter` (per user and IP, after `authorize`; added 2026-11-08), not by `authLimiter`. Response 201 `{ success, message: STAFF_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { ...staffUser, emailSent } }`; `tempPassword` is never in the response.

Coach flows: `POST /api/v1/admin/staff/coaches`: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `validateCreateCoach` → `staff.controller.createCoach` → `staff.service.createCoach` → shared `registerStaffUser` (role policy, duplicate-email check = 409 `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS`, temp password, ONE transaction: User + Coach + `STAFF_CREATED` audit, post-commit invitation email). Response 201 `{ success, message: COACH_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { ...fullCoachView, emailSent } }`. Reads and updates under `/api/v1/coaches`: `authenticate` → `authorize(...allowedRoles)` → per-role validation (`validateByRole`: query or body schema chosen from `req.user.role`) → `coach.controller` → `coach.service` (ownership, visibility, editable fields via `coach.access`) → `coach.repository`. Query is validated into `req.validatedQuery` (Express 5 `req.query` is read-only). Limiters (added 2026-11-08), after `authorize`: `coachReadLimiter` on the three GETs, `coachLimiter` on the two PATCHes; `GET /auth/me` and `PATCH /uploads/profile-image` use `profileLimiter`.

Coach weekly availability (SLAPP 66/111 S3): `GET /api/v1/coaches/:coachId/availability`: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER, COACH)` → `coachReadLimiter` → `validateCoachIdParam` (400) → `coachAvailability.controller.getWeeklyAvailability` → `coachAvailability.service.getWeeklyAvailability` (`findCoachById`, 404 `COACH_NOT_FOUND`; `coach.access.assertCanViewAvailability`: managers any coach, a coach only their own, another coach 403 `FORBIDDEN`) → `coachAvailability.repository.findWeekByCoachId` (two queries: days, then their slots; no N+1) → `coachAvailability.mapper.toWeeklyAvailabilityResponse`. `PUT` same path: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` (a coach is 403) → `coachLimiter` → `validateCoachIdParam` → `validateSaveWeeklyAvailabilityBody` (S2 schema: exactly 7 days) → `saveWeeklyAvailability` controller → service (`assertManager`, `findCoachById` 404, `assertNoOverlappingSlots` 400 with the day and both slots in the message, then ONE `$transaction`: `lockCoachAvailability` (advisory lock per coach), `findWeekByCoachId` (before), `replaceWeek` (deleteMany days, slots go by ON DELETE CASCADE, one `createMany` for days, one for slots), `createAuditLog` `COACH_AVAILABILITY_UPDATED`, `findWeekByCoachId` (read back)) → 200 `{ success, message: COACH_AVAILABILITY_SAVED_SUCCESS, data }`. Both return `data { coachId, timezone, isConfigured, updatedAt (ISO UTC or null), days[7] { dayOfWeek, isAvailable, slots[{ startTime, endTime }] } }`. Other methods: 405 `Allow: GET, PUT`. No other table is written.
Coach unavailability / leave (SLAPP 66/111 S4): `GET /api/v1/coaches/:coachId/unavailability`: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER, COACH)` → `coachReadLimiter` → `validateCoachIdParam` → `validateListUnavailabilityQuery` → `coachUnavailability.controller.listUnavailability` → `coachUnavailability.service.listUnavailability` (coach 404, then `assertCanViewAvailability`: manager or own profile, another coach 403) → `coachUnavailability.repository.listUnavailability` (count + page). `POST /:coachId/unavailability` and `PATCH|DELETE /:coachId/unavailability/:unavailabilityId`: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `coachLimiter` → param validators → body validator (POST / PATCH) → controller → service: `assertManager`, coach 404, then ONE `$transaction` (advisory lock per coach → leave looked up by id AND coach, 404 `UNAVAILABILITY_NOT_FOUND` → date rules → overlap check excluding the edited row, 409 `UNAVAILABILITY_OVERLAP` → write → audit row). After the commit POST and PATCH run the read-only `findAffectedSessions` (3 queries at most) and return the leave with `affectedSessions` / `affectedTotal`. No session, booking or appointment is written.

Membership flows (`/api/v1/memberships`):

- List (`GET /api/v1/memberships`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `membership.controller.listMemberships` → `membership.service.listMemberships` → `membership.repository.listWithPlans` (single Prisma query with `include: { plans: true }`, calculates `planCount = plans.length`, maps plans via `toMembershipPlanResponse`) → 200 `{ success, data: [{ id, name, description, createdAt, planCount, plans }] }`.
- Creation (`POST /api/v1/memberships`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validateCreateMembership` (Zod: trimmed `name` required, `description` optional) → `membership.controller.createMembership` → `membership.service.createMembership` (case-insensitive duplicate check via repository, catches P2002 race on raw SQL `LOWER(name)` index → 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`) → `membership.repository.create` → 201 `{ success, message: MEMBERSHIP_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { id, name, description, createdAt } }`.
- Other methods: `router.all` on `/api/v1/memberships` returns 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow: GET, POST`.

Plan list flow (`GET /api/v1/plans`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `validateListPlansQuery` (Zod, 400, unknown params stripped, result on `req.validatedQuery`) → `plan.controller.listPlans` → `plan.service.listPlans` (list query and the independent top-level `counts` query run in parallel via `Promise.all`; rows go through `toMembershipPlanResponse` then `pick(PLAN_LIST_ITEM_FIELDS)`) → `membershipPlan.repository` (`buildListWhere` + `listByColumn` or `listByNameOrder`, `countNonArchivedByCreditType`). Response 200 `{ success, data, pagination, counts }` (`pagination` = `{ page, limit, total, totalPages }`, `counts` = the two section tab badges).

Plan creation flow (`POST /api/v1/plans`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validateCreatePlan` (Zod; strips helper fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc`; checks mutual exclusivity between `membershipId` and `membership { name, description }` → 400 if both present) → `plan.controller.createPlan` → `plan.service.createPlan` (3 creation modes: Mode 1 existing membership verified via `membership.repository.findById`, else 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with an `errors[]` entry on `membershipId`; Mode 2 new membership + plan + audit row created in ONE atomic Prisma `$transaction` via `createPlanWithAudit`, catches duplicate membership name → 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`; Mode 3 standalone with `membershipId: null`) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/plans/:id`.

Plan detail and edit flows: `GET /api/v1/plans/:id`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `validatePlanIdParam` (400 for a non-UUID id) → `loadPlan()` (404) → `plan.controller.getPlan` → `plan.service.getPlan` (returns full record incl. `membershipId`, nested `membership: { id, name, description }` or null, `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`, `joiningFee`, `creditSummary`, `creditNote`, `activeClientCount`). `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validatePlanIdParam` (400) → `loadPlan({ rejectArchived: true })` (404, 409 `PLAN_ARCHIVED`) → `validateUpdatePlanBody` (400; rejects non-editable fields incl. `joiningFee`, rejects unknown fields incl. `membership` object, sets `req.validatedBody`) → `plan.controller.updatePlan` → `plan.service.updatePlan` (validates `membershipId` if sent: UUID verified to exist, else 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with an `errors[]` entry on `membershipId`; `null` reassigns to standalone; diff, `PLAN_DUPLICATE_NAME`, `PLAN_IN_USE`) → `updatePlanWithAudit` (one transaction; DB trigger bumps the version). `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id/status`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validatePlanIdParam` (400) → `loadPlan()` → `validatePlanStatusBody` (400) → `plan.controller.changePlanStatus` → `plan.service.changePlanStatus` (same-status shortcut, `PLAN_STATUS_TRANSITIONS`, restore-time name check; status changes never touch client assignments or memberships) → `changePlanStatusWithAudit` (one transaction, compare-and-set on the old status). `router.all` after the real routes answers 405 + `Allow` for every other method on `/:id` and `/:id/status`.

Class flows (`/api/v1/classes`):

- Listing (`GET /`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `classReadLimiter` → `validateListClassesQuery` (Zod, 400 on bad values; unknown stripped, result on `req.validatedQuery`) → `class.controller.listClasses` → `class.service.listClasses` (role-based filter: members see only active public; coaches see active public + assigned; staff default excludes archived, support status=active/deactivated/archived/all; legacy `active` query param mapped cleanly; batch loads with `CLASS_INCLUDE` and parallel `count`, no N+1) → `class.mapper.toClassResponse`.
- Detail (`GET /:id`): `authenticate` → `authorize(all roles)` → `classReadLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.getClass` → `class.service.getClass` (role visibility check: 404 for member if private or inactive; 404 for coach if private/inactive and unassigned; enriched with scheduleSummary, recurringSchedule, upcomingInstancesSummary) → `toClassResponse`.
- Creation (`POST /`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateCreateClass` (400; requires intensity 1-5, validates classType, classPassEnabled, coachIds; public requires >=1 eligible plan; plans checked active and existing) → `class.controller.createClass` → `class.service.createClass` (pre-checks name uniqueness against active classes, catches P2002 race → 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`, atomic class + coach + plan links in `$transaction`) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/classes/:id`.
- Update (`PATCH /:id`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `validateUpdateClassBody` (rejects non-editable `status`, `active`, `eligiblePlanIds`, `externalRef` and unknown keys with 400; merged state validation prevents DB check violations) → `class.controller.updateClass` → `class.service.updateClass` (rejects inactive or archived class → 409 `CLASS_INACTIVE`; private->public requires >=1 plan; name change uniqueness check + P2002 mapping; optimistic locking with `version` if provided → 409 `CLASS_VERSION_CONFLICT`) → `class.repository.updateClass` (executes set-based SQL UPDATE propagation for capacity and duration to future non-overridden sessions, updates coach links, bumps version).
- Deactivate (`POST /:id/deactivate`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.deactivateClass` → `class.service.deactivateClass` (idempotent if already deactivated; calls `countFutureBookedSessions` interim seam: throws 409 `CLASS_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS` with `details: { sessionCount }` if > 0; sets `status=DEACTIVATED`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'deactivated', active: false }`.
- Activate (`POST /:id/activate`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.activateClass` → `class.service.activateClass` (idempotent if already active; pre-checks active name collision and catches P2002 race → 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`; sets `status=ACTIVE`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'active', active: true }`.
- Archive (`POST /:id/archive`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.archiveClass` → `class.service.archiveClass` (idempotent if already archived; calls future bookings seam; sets `status=ARCHIVED`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'archived', active: false }`.
- Eligibility (`PUT /:id/eligibility`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateClassIdParam` (400) → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `validateUpdateEligibility` (400 body `{ planIds: [] }`) → `class.controller.updateEligibility` → `class.service.updateEligibility` (rejects inactive or archived class → 409 `CLASS_INACTIVE`; public with empty list → 400; validates plans exist and not archived; atomic transaction with `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, replaces links) → 200 `{ classId, planIds }`.
- Other methods: `router.all` on `/`, `/:id`, `/:id/deactivate`, `/:id/activate`, `/:id/archive`, `/:id/eligibility` returns 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow` header.

Cafe catalogue flows (`/api/v1/cafe`):

- Categories list (`GET /categories`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `cafeCategory.controller.getCategories` → `cafeCategory.service.getCategories` (counts public items only for MEMBER role, all items for staff) → 200 `{ success, data: [{ id, name, description, itemCount, createdAt, updatedAt }] }`.
- Category creation (`POST /categories`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCreateCategory` → `cafeCategory.controller.createCategory` → `cafeCategory.service.createCategory` (case-insensitive duplicate check → 409 `CATEGORY_EXISTS`) → 201 + created category.
- Category update & delete (`PATCH`, `DELETE /categories/:categoryId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCategoryIdParam` → update (`validateUpdateCategory`, 409 duplicate check) / delete (409 `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS` guard if any item references category).
- Items list (`GET /items`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateListItemsQuery` → `cafeItem.controller.listItems` → `cafeItem.service.listItems` (escaped search, category filter, member sees only `visibility=public` while sold_out items remain visible) → `toCafeItemResponse` (lowercase API enums, numbers for Decimals, nested category). Response 200 `{ success, data, pagination }`.
- Item creation (`POST /items`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCreateItem` → `cafeItem.controller.createItem` → `cafeItem.service.createItem` (tracked requires stock and unitCost, stock 0 forces `sold_out`, setting `available` with 0 stock -> 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`, untracked forces stock/cost to null) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/cafe/items/:id`.
- Item detail (`GET /items/:itemId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(all roles)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `cafeItem.controller.getItem` → `cafeItem.service.getItemById` (member querying `staff_only` item gets 404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`) → 200 `{ success, data }`.
- Item core update (`PATCH /items/:itemId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `validateUpdateItem` (rejects `availability` and `visibility` with 400 `ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE`) → `cafeItem.controller.patchItem` → `cafeItem.service.updateItem` (if tracked stock becomes 0, auto-sets `sold_out`).
- Item availability & visibility updates: `PATCH /items/:itemId/availability`: `cafeLimiter` → `validateUpdateItemAvailability` → `patchItemAvailability` → `cafeItem.service.updateItemAvailability` (setting `available` with 0 stock -> 400). `PATCH /items/:itemId/visibility`: `cafeLimiter` → `validateUpdateItemVisibility` → `patchItemVisibility` → `cafeItem.service.updateItemVisibility`.
- Item stock (`PATCH /items/:itemId/stock`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `validateUpdateItemStock` (required integer `quantity` >= 0, no string coercion -> 400) → `cafeInventory.controller.patchItemStock` → `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` (404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`, 409 `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED`, direct overwrite, `SOLD_OUT` in the same write at 0, restock keeps availability) → 200 `{ success, message: ITEM_STOCK_UPDATED_SUCCESS, data: toCafeStockUpdateResponse view }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: PATCH`.
- Inventory list (`GET /inventory`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateListInventoryQuery` (search/categoryId/page/limit as the items list plus uppercase `status`; invalid -> 400) → `cafeInventory.controller.listInventory` → `cafeInventory.service.listInventory` (tracked only incl. `staff_only`, name ASC, status filtered before paging) → 200 `{ success, data: toCafeInventoryItemResponse[], pagination }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: GET`.
- Inventory summary (`GET /inventory/summary`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `cafeInventory.controller.getInventorySummary` → `cafeInventory.service.getSummary` (tracked only incl. `staff_only`; no query) → 200 `{ success, data: { trackedItems, lowStock, outOfStock, stockValueAed, itemsMissingCost, itemsSold: null, revenueFromItemsAed: null, bestSeller: null, salesDataAvailable: false } }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: GET`.
- Item image upload token (`POST /uploads/image/token`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCafeImageToken` (Zod `image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp` -> 400 on bad MIME) → `issueCafeImageToken` → `createImageUploadToken(contentType, 'cafe-items')` → 200 `{ success, message, data: { clientToken, expiresAt, maxSizeBytes, allowedContentTypes, upload: { method, url, headers } } }`.
- Disallowed methods: `router.all` answers 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow` header (`GET /items/:itemId` rejects `DELETE` with 405; items are never hard-deleted).

Cafe order flows (`/api/v1/cafe/orders`, SLAPP-79/82; reads in phase 1, status change in phase 2):

- Order list (`GET /orders`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` (`ORDER_READ_ROLES`) → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateListOrdersQuery` (strict: unknown params 400) → `cafeOrder.controller.listCafeOrders` → `cafeOrder.service.listOrders` (`buildOrderScope(req.user)`: OWNER/ADMIN all, others `createdById = req.user.id`) → `cafeOrder.repository.listOrders` / `countOrders`.
- Order summary (`GET /orders/summary`, registered before `/orders/:orderId`): same chain with `validateOrderSummaryQuery` (only `from`/`to`) → `getCafeOrderSummary` → `cafeOrder.service.getSummary` → `cafeOrder.repository.getOrderSummary` (same scope).
- Order detail (`GET /orders/:orderId`): same chain with `validateOrderIdParam` → `validateOrderDetailQuery` (no query params allowed) → `getCafeOrder` → `cafeOrder.service.getOrder` (404 `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` for missing and out-of-scope orders).
- Other methods on the 3 read paths: 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow: GET`.
- Status change (`PATCH /orders/:orderId/status`, phase 2): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` (`ORDER_ADMIN_ROLES`) → `cafeLimiter` → `validateOrderIdParam` (400) → `validateOrderDetailQuery` (no query params) → `validateUpdateOrderStatusBody` (strict `{ status }`, 400) → `cafeOrder.controller.patchCafeOrderStatus` → `cafeOrder.service.updateOrderStatus` (re-checks admin role; `findOrderStatusById` 404; transition check 409 `INVALID_ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITION`) → `cafeOrder.repository.updateOrderStatusWithAudit` (`$transaction`: conditional `updateMany`, `createAuditLog`, detail re-read; `null` on a lost race → re-read → 404 or 409). Other methods: 405 with `Allow: PATCH`.

---

## 3. File Index

### `src/` — root

| Path            | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Exports       |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------- |
| `src/app.js`    | Express app factory: disables `x-powered-by`, sets `trust proxy` from `env.trustProxy`, mounts helmet, cors, `clientType`, pino-http, `express.json({ limit: '1mb' })`, `express.urlencoded`, routes, notFound, errorHandler. CORS options: `*` in dev/test (when `env.corsOrigins === '*'`), otherwise an origin callback that allows requests with NO `Origin` header (native mobile, curl, server-to-server) and origins listed in `env.corsOrigins`; an unlisted origin gets no CORS headers (browser blocks it, no error response). Methods GET/POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE/OPTIONS, allowed headers `Content-Type` + `Authorization`, `credentials: false` (no cookies), preflight `maxAge` 600s. pino-http skips auto-logging for `/health*` and `customProps` adds `clientType` to every request log | `createApp()` |
| `src/server.js` | Entry point: connects Prisma, starts HTTP server on `env.PORT`, graceful shutdown on SIGTERM/SIGINT/unhandledRejection/uncaughtException (10s force-exit timeout)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | —             |

### Project root & `prisma/`

| Path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `package.json`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Deps & npm scripts (see §1); `main` = `src/server.js`; `engines.node` ≥ 20                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `jest.config.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `testEnvironment: node`, roots `tests/`, `clearMocks: true`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `eslint.config.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Flat config: recommended + prettier; `no-console` error, `no-unused-vars` (ignore `_` args), `eqeqeq`, `prefer-const`, `no-var`; jest globals for `tests/**`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `.prettierrc`, `.prettierignore`, `.editorconfig`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Formatting config (Prettier wants LF line endings)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `.env.example`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Template of env vars validated by `env.js` (no secrets; includes empty `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN=`, the 4 seed-only `SEED_OWNER_*` vars; comments explain the `CORS_ORIGINS` production rule with a placeholder list `https://app.your-domain.example,…`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `.env`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Local env (gitignored); also used by jest — there is no separate test env file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `README.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Title only ("Backend")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `prisma/schema.prisma`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Prisma schema (see §4). Datasource reads `url = env("DATABASE_URL")` and `directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `prisma/seed.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Seed entry (`npm run prisma:seed`): loads the real `env`/prisma client, calls `seedOwner(process.env)`, prints `describeSeedResult` to stdout; on failure prints the full message only for `SEED_CONFIG_INVALID` errors, otherwise just error name + code (Prisma errors can embed submitted data), sets exit code 1, always disconnects. Never prints the password                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `prisma/seed-owner.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Testable owner-seed logic (no I/O besides repository calls). `seedOwner(source)`: validates the 4 `SEED_OWNER_*` vars with the signup rules (`emailField`, `fullNameField`, `phoneField`, `PASSWORD_REGEX` + max 72; issue messages never echo values) → throws `createAppError(400, …, 'SEED_CONFIG_INVALID')` listing every bad/missing var; `findByEmail` → existing OWNER = `ALREADY_EXISTS`, existing non-OWNER = `EXISTS_WITH_OTHER_ROLE` (nothing changed either way: no password overwrite, no role change); otherwise `createUser` with `crypto.randomUUID()`, role OWNER, status ACTIVE, `mustChangePassword` false, gender `STAFF_DEFAULT_GENDER`, `hashPassword` hash. No audit row written (decision: default none). Exports `seedOwner`, `describeSeedResult(result)`, `SeedResult` (CREATED/ALREADY_EXISTS/EXISTS_WITH_OTHER_ROLE), `SEED_CONFIG_INVALID`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `prisma/migrations/20261001183740_create_user_table/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | User table + Role/AccountStatus/Gender enums                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `prisma/migrations/20261003201244_add_emergency_contact_and_waiver/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | EmergencyContact table, Relationship enum, waiver/registration-completed fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `prisma/migrations/20261004053218_make_emergency_contact_name_relationship_optional/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | EmergencyContact `name`/`relationship` nullable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Includes `down.sql` (run manually; drops trigger, function, both tables and both enums; round-trip verified locally). **Not applied to Neon**; verified with `prisma migrate deploy` on a local Postgres 16 |

Not touched by SLAPP 66/111. |
| `prisma/migrations/20261111100000_add_coach_availability/` | SLAPP 66/111 S1 (2026-11-11): tables `coach_availability_days` (PK `(coachId, dayOfWeek)`, `isAvailable`), `coach_availability_slots` (`startTime` / `endTime` `TIME(0)`, composite FK `(coachId, dayOfWeek)` to the day row ON DELETE CASCADE, unique `(coachId, dayOfWeek, startTime)`) and `coach_unavailability` (`startDate` / `endDate` `DATE`, `reason`, nullable `note`, indexes `(coachId, startDate, endDate)` and `(startDate, endDate)`); all three FK to `Coach` ON DELETE CASCADE; 7 CHECK constraints (`coach_availability_days_day_check` 1-7, `coach_availability_slots_day_check` 1-7, `coach_availability_slots_time_order_check` `endTime > startTime`, `coach_unavailability_date_order_check` `endDate >= startDate`, `coach_unavailability_reason_check` not blank and <= 200, `coach_unavailability_note_check` NULL or <= 1000); `AuditAction` + 5 values. `down.sql` drops the 3 tables, DELETES audit rows that use the 5 new values, rebuilds the `AuditAction` enum without them and removes its `_prisma_migrations` row. Tested up, constraints, down, re-up on a local Postgres 18. Overlap between slots of a day, and between leave ranges of a coach, is NOT a DB rule: the service rejects it (decision: no `btree_gist`). **Not applied to Neon.** |

Emits warnings for derived cycles and 1-credit single_visit candidates, `runSeed`, `GLOFOX_SEED_CONFIG`, `parseDuration`, `parseCreditsIncluded`, `deriveBillingCycle`, `deriveCreditRefill`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `prisma/seeds/classes.seed.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | SL-CLS-01 seed (`npm run seed:classes`, optional file argument or `SEED_CLASSES_FILE`): reads sheets "Classes" and "Weekly Timetable" (header row 4), folds spelling variants of one programme into one (`programmeKey`, `collectProgrammes`), folds timetable rows into slots (`collectSlots`), validates classes with Zod, saves each programme with its coach links and slots in ONE transaction (`saveProgramme`, which passes `data.durationMin` to `repository.upsertSlot` so new slots get `endTime`; `startDate` defaults to today), prints a report with warnings (`DUPLICATE_PROGRAMME`, `VISIBILITY_CONFLICT`, `COACH_NOT_FOUND`, `UNKNOWN_CLASS_IN_TIMETABLE`) and the public classes with zero eligible plans. Exports `runSeed`, `CLASSES_SEED_CONFIG`, `WarningType`, `parseDurationMin`, `parseCapacities`, `programmeKey`, `parseDayOfWeek`, `parseStartTime`, `collectProgrammes`, `collectSlots`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### `src/config/`

| Path                      | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Exports                        |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------ |
| `src/config/env.js`       | Loads dotenv, validates env vars via Zod (exits on failure with a per-variable issue list), validates SMTP vars in non-test mode, accepts optional `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN` (no default, not required; server-only, never returned to clients), validates `CORS_ORIGINS` (bare-origin format; wildcard/empty rejected when `NODE_ENV=production`), validates `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` (min 32, placeholder values rejected) and `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN` (number + s/m/h/d), accepts `REFRESH_SESSION_CLEANUP_GRACE_DAYS`, `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, freezes result with derived `refreshRateLimitMax` (var, else 60, else 100000 in test), `isDev`, `isProd`, `isTest`, `corsOrigins` (`'*'` or a trimmed, lowercased array), `trustProxy`, and exposes `schema` | `env` (object, default export) |
| `src/config/logger.js`    | Pino logger (pino-pretty in dev, silent in test); redacts `req.headers.authorization`, `req.headers.cookie`, `password`, `*.password`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `logger` (default export)      |
| `src/config/prisma.js`    | Singleton PrismaClient (logs warn/error in dev, error otherwise)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `prisma` (default export)      |
| `src/config/container.js` | Dependency container                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | (empty placeholder)            |

### `src/middlewares/`

| Path                                             | Purpose
