--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/middlewares/errorHandler.middleware.js`     | Global 4-arg error handler. Every body is `{ success: false, message, code, errors?, details? }`: malformed JSON→400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`, ZodError→400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `errors[{field,message}]`, Prisma P2002→409 (`USER_ALREADY_EXISTS`), Postgres RESTRICT violation (`isRestrictViolationError`)→409 `RESOURCE_IN_USE`, operational AppError→own status, its own `code`, and forwards optional `details` object (e.g. `sessionCount` on `CLASS_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS`), else→500 generic with `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR`; logs 5xx/non-operational. The rate limiter (429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`) and `notFound` (404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`) reach it through `createAppError`, so they carry their code too. **2026-11-08:** body over the 1 MB limit -> 413 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` with a code; the malformed-JSON text is `Messages.MALFORMED_JSON`; an unmapped Prisma P2002 is now a generic 409 `RESOURCE_ALREADY_EXISTS` (it used to say "User already exists" for every table; services that know the record throw their own error first) and an unmapped P2025 is 404 `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND`; ZodError and `sendValidationFailed` share `toFieldErrors`, so an error about the whole body/query has `field: "body"` (it was an empty string).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `errorHandler` (default)                                                                                                                                        |
| `src/middlewares/notFound.middleware.js`         | Forwards 404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND` (`AppErrors.ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`, fixed message "The requested route does not exist") for unmatched routes. The message no longer echoes the method, path or query string (changed 2026-11-08). `app.js` mounts it behind `publicLimiter`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `notFound` (default)                                                                                                                                            |
| `src/middlewares/loadPlan.middleware.js`         | `loadPlan({ rejectArchived = false })` factory (run after `authenticate` + `authorize`): loads the plan named by `req.params.id` with `findById` onto `req.plan`; unknown or malformed id → 404 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND` (the id column is TEXT, so a malformed id is just not found and the id format is not revealed); `rejectArchived` → 409 `PLAN_ARCHIVED` **2026-11-08:** a malformed id never reaches it any more: `validatePlanIdParam` answers 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` first, so here only a well-formed unknown id is 404 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `loadPlan`                                                                                                                                                      |
| `src/middlewares/loadClass.middleware.js`        | `loadClass()` middleware (runs after `authenticate` + `authorize`): loads class named by `req.params.id` via `findById` onto `req.class`; unknown or malformed id → 404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND` **2026-11-08:** same as `loadPlan`: `validateClassIdParam` / `validateSlotIdParams` (400) run before it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `loadClass`                                                                                                                                                     |
| `src/middlewares/methodNotAllowed.middleware.js` | `methodNotAllowed(allowedMethods)` returns a middleware LIST `[publicLimiter, handler]` (changed 2026-11-08): the IP rate limit first, then the handler that sets `Allow` and passes 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` to the error handler. Used with `router.all` after the real routes of every router (plans, memberships, classes, slots, sessions, cafe and, since 2026-11-08, auth, staff, coaches, uploads and health), so a wrong method on a real path is 405, never 404. It runs before `authenticate`, so it never needs a token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `methodNotAllowed`                                                                                                                                              |
| `src/middlewares/validate.middleware.js`         | Validation factories (each forwards the ZodError on failure): `validateBody(schema)` replaces `req.body` with the parsed data (unknowns stripped unless the schema is strict); `validateParams(schema)` replaces `req.params`; `validateQuery(schema)` stores the parsed query on `req.validatedQuery` (Express 5 `req.query` is read-only); `validateByRole(validatorsByRole)` runs the validator of `req.user.role` (after `authenticate`) and answers 403 `FORBIDDEN` for a role without an entry **2026-11-08:** `validateBody` answers 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `errors: [{ field: "body", message: "Request body is required" }]` when the request has no body at all (`req.body === undefined`), instead of Zod's "expected object, received undefined".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `validateBody`, `validateParams`, `validateQuery`, `validateByRole`                                                                                             |
| `src/middlewares/authenticate.middleware.js`     | Bearer-JWT guard. Reads `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (scheme case-insensitive; missing/malformed → 401 `UNAUTHORIZED` with `NO_TOKEN_PROVIDED` message), `verifyAccessToken` (`TokenExpiredError` → 401 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`, other `JsonWebTokenError` → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, non-jwt errors rethrown), loads user by `sub` via `findAuthById` (missing `sub` or user not found → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, so existence is not revealed), `assertCanLogin` for status (PENDING → 403 `REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE`, DISABLED → 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`), sets `req.user = { id, role, status }` from the DB (token role ignored). All errors via `next(err)`. No mustChangePassword check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `authenticate`                                                                                                                                                  |
| `src/middlewares/authorize.middleware.js`        | `authorize(...allowedRoles)` factory: throws `Error("authorize: unknown role …")` at registration if an argument is not a `Role` value; at request time no `req.user` → 401 `UNAUTHORIZED`; `req.user.role` not in allowedRoles → logs one `logger.warn` (`AUTHORIZATION_DENIED`: userId, role, method, URL without query, ipAddress, clientType, ISO timestamp) and returns 403 `FORBIDDEN`; else `next()`. Must run after `authenticate`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `authorize(...allowedRoles)`                                                                                                                                    |
| `src/middlewares/clientType.middleware.js`       | Sets `req.clientType` (`web` \| `mobile` \| `unknown`) from the `user-agent` and `origin` headers via `detectClientType`, using `env.corsOrigins` as the allow-list; always calls `next()`. Logging/analytics only: used by pino-http `customProps`, NOT stored in the DB (no sessions/audit columns), NOT a security signal                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `clientType` (default)                                                                                                                                          |
| `src/middlewares/rateLimiter.middleware.js`      | express-rate-limit factory using `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`/`RATE_LIMIT_MAX` (defaults 15 min / **1000**; in test env the max defaults to 100000 unless `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` is set in `process.env`; an `options.max` / `options.windowMs` overrides both); standard `RateLimit-*` headers on, legacy `X-RateLimit-*` off; over-limit → `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` 429 via `next(err)`. Store is in-memory (per process, per IP). `authLimiter` is one shared instance (one per-IP counter across all auth routes using it) Plan limiters (SL-MP-02/03): `createPlanLimiter(options?)` keys by `${req.user.id}_${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip)}` (IP only when unauthenticated); `planLimiter` (POST /plans) and `planReadLimiter` (GET /plans) are two separate instances. Class limiters (SL-CLS-02): `classLimiter` (POST/PATCH/PUT/deactivate) and `classReadLimiter` (GET /classes) instances created via `createPlanLimiter`, placed after `authorize` so 403 does not consume budget. Cafe limiters (SLAPP-79/80): `cafeLimiter` (cafe writes and the image token) and `cafeReadLimiter` (cafe reads), two more `createPlanLimiter()` instances with no custom options, so they use the same defaults as every other limiter (`RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS` / `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, default max 1000; 100000 in test). SLAPP-79/82: the 3 order reads (`GET /cafe/orders`, `/orders/summary`, `/orders/:orderId`) also use `cafeReadLimiter` and share its counter with the other cafe reads; the phase 2 `PATCH /cafe/orders/:orderId/status` uses `cafeLimiter` (shared with the other cafe writes). Scheduling (BE-SCH): slot and session routes reuse `classLimiter` / `classReadLimiter`. All export from the module. **2026-11-08 (every route is limited now):** new `createPlanLimiter()` instances `staffLimiter` (both `POST /admin/staff*`), `coachLimiter` (coach PATCHes) and `coachReadLimiter` (coach GETs), `profileLimiter` (`GET /auth/me`, `PATCH /uploads/profile-image`), all after `authenticate` + `authorize`; and `publicLimiter` (`createAuthLimiter()`, IP only, ONE shared counter) for `/health/ready`, the whole `/api-docs` router, the 404 fallback and every 405 answer. `GET /health` (liveness) is the only unlimited route. Same default 1000 per 15 minutes; nothing existing changed. Guarded by `tests/unit/routes.rate-limit-coverage.test.js`, which walks the routers and fails for any route without a limiter. | `createAuthLimiter`, `authLimiter`, `createPlanLimiter`, `planLimiter`, `planReadLimiter`, `classLimiter`, `classReadLimiter`, `cafeLimiter`, `cafeReadLimiter` |

Shape matches `createAuditLog` `meta`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `getRequestMeta(req)` → `{ ipAddress, userAgent }`                                                                                         |
| `src/utils/clientType.util.js`      | Pure client classifier. `detectClientType({ userAgent, origin, allowedOrigins })`, in order: (1) `Origin` present (trimmed, lowercased) and in `allowedOrigins` (or `allowedOrigins === '*'`) → `web`; (2) `Origin` present but not allowed → `unknown` (even with an OkHttp UA, so a foreign/spoofed origin is never trusted); (3) no Origin + UA word match `okhttp`, `retrofit`, `ktor`, `dalvik`, `apache-httpclient` (Kotlin/Android stacks) → `mobile`; (4) no Origin + UA starting `Mozilla/` (browsers, same-origin/navigation) and not containing `node`/`undici`/`node-fetch` → `web`; (5) otherwise (curl, Postman, Node/Next.js server-side fetch, empty/non-string UA) → `unknown`. Both headers are spoofable outside a browser: never use for auth | `ClientType` (`WEB`, `MOBILE`, `UNKNOWN`), `detectClientType`                                                                              |
| `src/utils/html.util.js`            | HTML escaping for user-supplied text placed in HTML (email bodies). `escapeHtml(value)`: converts the value to a string and replaces `& < > " '` with entities (single regex pass, so `&` is never double-escaped). Used for `fullName` in `notify.service` html bodies                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `escapeHtml(value)`                                                                                                                        |

| `src/controllers/auth.controller.js`            | Auth HTTP handlers (thin). `me`: calls `getProfile(req.user.id)`, 200 `PROFILE_FETCHED_SUCCESS` with `data: { user }`; errors → `next(err)`. `logout`: calls the service's `logout(req.body.refreshToken, req.user, getRequestMeta(req))` (imported as `logoutSession`), always 200 `LOGOUT_SUCCESS` with `data: null` for an authenticated caller; errors → `next(err)`. `refresh`: calls `refreshTokens(req.body.refreshToken, getRequestMeta(req))`, 200 `TOKEN_REFRESH_SUCCESS` with `data: { tokens }` (same token fields as login); errors → `next(err)`. `resetPassword`: calls the service's `resetPassword({ email, resetToken, newPassword, meta })`, 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_SUCCESS` with `data: null`. `verifyOtp`: calls `verifyResetOtp({ email, otp, meta })`, 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED_SUCCESS` with `data: { resetToken, expiresAt }`. `forgotPassword`: calls `requestPasswordReset({ email: req.body.email, meta: getRequestMeta(req) })`, always 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_REQUESTED` with `data: null` (identical for every email). `register`: calls `registerMember(req.body)`, 201 `REGISTER_SUCCESS` with the public user. `completeRegister`: calls `completeMemberRegistration(req.body)`, 200 `REGISTRATION_COMPLETE_SUCCESS`. `login`: builds meta `{ ipAddress, userAgent }` via `getRequestMeta` (imported from `utils/requestMeta.util.js`), calls `loginWithPassword(req.body, meta)`, responds 200 `Messages.LOGIN_SUCCESS` with `data: { user, tokens }` via `sendSuccess`; errors → `next(err)` `resetPassword`                                                                                                     |
| `src/services/auth.service.js`                  | Business logic: `getProfile(userId)` (`findPublicById` → `toPublicUser`; missing user → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`), `registerMember(input)` (order: `findByEmail` → 409; `hashPassword`; `createUser` with `profileImageUrl ?? null` — the URL was already validated by `registerSchema`; no upload/delete happens here; creates PENDING MEMBER with a `crypto.randomUUID()` id), `completeMemberRegistration` (`findById` → `runGuards(registrationGuards)` → `completeRegistrationTx` → `toCompletedRegistration`), `loginWithPassword({ email, password }, meta)`: `findForLogin` → bcrypt compare against `user?.passwordHash ?? lazy dummy hash` (dummy = hash of a random UUID, created once with the real cost factor) → on unknown email/wrong password `recordFailureSafely` (reason INVALID_CREDENTIALS, userId only if user exists) + 401 `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` → only then `assertCanLogin` (on 403 records ACCOUNT_PENDING for PENDING, ACCOUNT_DISABLED for anything else, rethrows) → `startLoginSession` (refresh token + SHA-256 hash, `expiresAt = now + REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`, `createLoginSessionTx`, `signAccessToken`) → returns `{ user: toPublicUser(user), tokens: { accessToken, accessTokenExpiresIn, refreshToken, refreshTokenExpiresAt } }`. `recordFailureSafely` swallows audit-write errors and logs only `{ reason, userId, errorName, errorCode }` with `Messages.LOGIN_AUDIT_WRITE_FAILED` (never the error message, password, hash or tokens), so the HTTP response never changes. `refreshTokens(refreshToken, meta)` (refresh ticket; exposed as `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh` since part 2b): `findUsableSession` → `findByTokenHash(hashToken(token))`; unknown → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; **revoked → reuse**: `rejectReusedToken` = `revokeAllByUser(userId)` then 401 `INVALID_TOKEN` (checked before expiry, no `replacedBy` column); expired → 401 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`. Then `loadUserForRefresh` → `findAuthWithEmailById` (missing → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; `assertCanLogin` → 403 for PENDING/DISABLED/unknown status, sessions are not revoked in that case). Then new `generateRefreshToken`, `expiresAt = now + REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`, `rotate(session.id, { userId, tokenHash: hashToken(new), expiresAt, meta }, now)`; `null` (another request used the token first) → plain 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, NO revoke-all (changed in part 2b, so a double-click does not log the user out everywhere; a revoked token presented later is still reuse). Returns the same shape as login `tokens` (`accessToken` signed with the DB email + role, `accessTokenExpiresIn`, `refreshToken`, `refreshTokenExpiresAt`). No audit row, no `lastLoginAt` update, DB errors propagate (500). `logout(refreshToken, user, meta)` (user = `req.user`): `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx({ tokenHash: hashToken(token), userId: user.id, meta })`; the session is revoked and one `LOGOUT` audit row written (same transaction) only if the token belongs to the authenticated user and is still active; unknown, foreign, already revoked tokens do nothing and write nothing, and the response is identical (idempotent, no information leak, no revoke-all); a DISABLED/PENDING user cannot call it (`authenticate` → 403); DB errors propagate (500). Does not reuse `startLoginSession` (it writes login side effects) **2026-11-08:** `registerMember` throws `AppErrors.USER_ALREADY_EXISTS` (409 with code `USER_ALREADY_EXISTS`; the old raw 409 had no code on the wire), also for a parallel request that loses the unique race on `createUser`. | `registerMember(input)`, `completeMemberRegistration(input)`, `loginWithPassword(input, meta)`, `refreshTokens(refreshToken, meta)`, `logout(refreshToken, user, meta)`                                          |

| `src/validators/auth.validation.js`             | Zod schemas. `registerSchema.profileImageUrl` uses `trustedImageUrl.optional()` imported from `uploads.validation` (shared; same rules as before). Internal helpers: `phoneField`, `optionalIntField`, `emailField` (shared by register + login + staff: trim, lowercase, max 254, email format), `fullNameField` (trim, 2–100). Every error text comes from `Messages` (no inline literals; the password policy text still comes from `PASSWORD_POLICY_MESSAGE` in `password.util.js`). registerSchema (fullNameField, emailField, E.164-style phones, optional `whatsappNumber`, `gender`, optional `birthDay`/`birthMonth` (both or neither, day checked against the month's max, Feb 29 allowed), optional `profileImageUrl` — ""/null/blank → absent, trimmed string ≤ 2048 chars that must pass `isTrustedImageUrl`, error `IMAGE_URL_INVALID` — password policy regex, max 72), completeRegistrationSchema (`registrationId` UUID, `emergencyContact` with required phone and optional name/relationship, `waiverAccepted` must be literal `true`), `refreshTokenSchema` (also used as-is by logout; `{ refreshToken }`: string, trimmed, min 1, missing/blank/non-string → `REFRESH_TOKEN_REQUIRED`; unknown keys stripped; no format check on purpose, an unknown token is a 401 not a 400), forgotPasswordSchema (`{ email: emailField }`), verifyOtpSchema (`email: emailField`; `otp`: string, trimmed, must match `^\d{OTP_LENGTH}$` (4 digits, built from `OTP_LENGTH` in `otp.util`) — missing/non-string → `OTP_REQUIRED`, wrong format incl. non-ASCII digits → `OTP_FORMAT_INVALID`, leading zeros kept), resetPasswordSchema (`email: emailField`; `resetToken`: string, trimmed, min 1 → `RESET_TOKEN_REQUIRED`; `newPassword`: shared `passwordField` = string, max 72, `PASSWORD_REGEX` with `PASSWORD_POLICY_MESSAGE`, missing → `PASSWORD_REQUIRED`); `passwordField` is also used by registerSchema; all three new schemas strip unknown keys; loginSchema (emailField; password string 1–72 with `Messages.PASSWORD_REQUIRED` / `PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH`, **no** policy regex so rules don't leak and older passwords still work)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `{ registerSchema, completeRegistrationSchema, loginSchema, refreshTokenSchema, forgotPasswordSchema, verifyOtpSchema, resetPasswordSchema, fullNameField, emailField, phoneField }`                             |
| `src/services/password-reset.service.js`        | Password-reset business logic. `requestPasswordReset({ email, meta })` (email already normalized by `emailField`): `findByEmail` → `getSkipReason(user)` (private: no user → `UNKNOWN_EMAIL`; ACTIVE → null; PENDING → `ACCOUNT_PENDING`; any other status → `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, deny by default). Skipped: one `createAuditLog(prisma, { action: PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED, reason, email, meta })` (userId NULL, also for existing PENDING/DISABLED users), no OTP, no email, resolves `undefined`. ACTIVE: `generateOtp` → `hashPassword(otp)` (bcrypt) → `expiresAt = now + OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES` → `createOtpTx` (invalidates old OTPs, stores the new one, writes the `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` audit row, all in one tx) → `sendOtpInBackground` (private): `sendPasswordResetOtp(...)` is NOT awaited; a rejection is caught and logged as `{ userId, errorName, errorCode }` with `PASSWORD_RESET_EMAIL_FAILED` (never the OTP, the email address or the error message). Resolves `undefined` for known and unknown emails. A DB error in lookup/audit/`createOtpTx` propagates (500). No cooldown (not in the ticket). `verifyResetOtp({ email, otp, meta })`: `findLatestByEmail` → a row whose user is missing or not ACTIVE is replaced by `null` → `runGuards(verifyOtpGuards, { otp, now, maxAttempts: env.OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS })` (null → `OTP_INVALID`, so unknown email / PENDING / DISABLED look exactly like a wrong OTP; used, already verified, attempts reached, invalidated, expired → their own errors, with NO bcrypt compare, no attempt count and no audit) → `comparePassword(otp, row.otpHash)`. Wrong → private `failWrongOtp(row, meta)`: `incrementAttempts` (atomic) → if `attempts >= OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` also `invalidateOtp` and error = `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED` (429), else `OTP_INVALID` (400) → `createAuditLog(prisma, { userId, action: PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_FAILED, reason: <error code>, email, meta })` (the submitted OTP is never stored) → throws. Correct → `generateRefreshToken()` as `resetToken` → `markVerifiedTx({ otpId, userId, email, resetTokenHash: hashToken(resetToken), now, meta })` (token hash + `OTP_VERIFIED` audit in one transaction) → returns `{ resetToken, expiresAt: row.expiresAt }` (the raw token only exists in this return value; neither it nor the OTP is logged). Response time differs between unknown email (guard throws) and wrong OTP (bcrypt compare), accepted by the user. `resetPassword({ email, resetToken, newPassword, meta })`: same lookup (private `findLatestOtpOfActiveUser(email)`, shared with `verifyResetOtp`: missing or non-ACTIVE user → `null` → `OTP_INVALID`) → `runGuards(resetPasswordGuards, { otp, now, resetTokenHash: hashToken(resetToken) })` → `hashPassword(newPassword)` → `resetPasswordTx` (password + `mustChangePassword = false`, active `RefreshSession`s revoked, OTP `usedAt`, `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED` audit, one transaction); returns nothing; nothing sensitive logged or audited. Known gap: already issued access JWTs stay valid until they expire (`JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`) because `authenticate` does not check sessions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `{ requestPasswordReset, verifyResetOtp, resetPassword }`                                                                                                                                                        |
| `src/services/otp.guards.js`                    | Chain of Responsibility for password-reset OTPs (same style as `auth.guards`: sync guards taking a context `{ otp, now, maxAttempts, resetTokenHash }`, throw `createAppError(AppErrors.X)` or return; run with `runGuards` imported from `auth.guards`). `otp` = latest `PasswordResetOtp` row or null. Guards: `ensureOtpExists` (null → `OTP_INVALID`, same as a wrong OTP so unknown emails are not revealed), `ensureNotUsed` (`usedAt` → `OTP_ALREADY_USED`), `ensureNotVerified` (`verifiedAt` → `OTP_ALREADY_USED`; verify chain only, so an OTP cannot mint a second reset token), `ensureAttemptsLeft` (`attempts >= maxAttempts` → `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED` 429), `ensureNotInvalidated` (`invalidatedAt` → `OTP_INVALID`), `ensureNotExpired` (`expiresAt <= now` → `OTP_EXPIRED`), `ensureVerified` (no `verifiedAt` → `OTP_INVALID`), `ensureResetTokenMatches` (`otp.resetTokenHash !== resetTokenHash` → `OTP_INVALID`; both are SHA-256 hashes). Chains: `verifyOtpGuards` = exists → not used → not verified → attempts left → not invalidated → not expired; `resetPasswordGuards` = exists → not used → verified → not invalidated → not expired → token matches. `ensureAttemptsLeft` runs before `ensureNotInvalidated` so an OTP closed for too many attempts reports `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED`. The async bcrypt OTP comparison and the account-status check (only ACTIVE users) are NOT guards: the service does them. Used by `password-reset.service.js:23` (`verifyOtpGuards` and `resetPasswordGuards`, run with `runGuards`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `ensureOtpExists`, `ensureNotUsed`, `ensureNotVerified`, `ensureAttemptsLeft`, `ensureNotInvalidated`, `ensureNotExpired`, `ensureVerified`, `ensureResetTokenMatches`, `verifyOtpGuards`, `resetPasswordGuards` |
| `src/services/login.policies.js`                | Strategy pattern for login by account status. Private `Map` status → policy: ACTIVE → allow (no throw), PENDING → throws `AppErrors.REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE` (403), DISABLED → throws `AppErrors.ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (403); any other/missing status → `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (deny by default, never allow). Uses a `Map` so inherited keys (`constructor`, `__proto__`) cannot match a policy. To add a status: add one map entry. Called by `loginWithPassword` after the password check and by `authenticate`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `assertCanLogin(user)` (map is not exported)                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `src/repositories/session.repository.js`        | Refresh-session DB operations (all take/return HASHES only; hashing is done by callers with `token.util.hashToken`). `findByTokenHash(tokenHash)` → `{ id, userId, expiresAt, revokedAt }` or null (does NOT check revoked/expired: the caller must). `revokeById(id, now = new Date())` → `updateMany` where `revokedAt` is null; returns `true` if it revoked the session, `false` if already revoked/unknown (never overwrites an earlier `revokedAt`). `revokeAllByUser(userId, now = new Date(), client = prisma)` → revokes all active sessions of the user, returns the count (`client` may be a transaction client; `setCoachStatus` revokes inside its transaction). `rotate(oldSessionId, { userId, tokenHash, expiresAt, meta? }, now = new Date())`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — conditional `updateMany({ id, revokedAt: null })` revokes the old session; if 0 rows matched it returns `null` (nothing written, so of two parallel refreshes with the same token exactly one wins), otherwise creates the new session inline (app UUID, hash only, ip/UA from `meta`, NULL when absent; no `lastLoginAt`, no audit row) and returns `{ sessionId }`; a failing insert (e.g. duplicate hash, P2002) rolls the revoke back. `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx({ tokenHash, userId, now = new Date(), meta = {} })`: ONE `$transaction` (options `ROTATE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS`) — `updateMany where { tokenHash, userId, revokedAt: null }` sets `revokedAt` (so an unknown token, another user's token and an already revoked token all match 0 rows); 0 rows → returns `false`, nothing else written, no audit row; otherwise `tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow` reads the email, `createAuditLog(tx, { userId, action: LOGOUT, email, meta })`, returns `true` (an audit failure rolls the revoke back). Used by `logout` in `auth.service.js`. `deleteStaleSessions(graceMs, now = new Date())`: `deleteMany` where `expiresAt < now - graceMs` OR `revokedAt < now - graceMs` (a null `revokedAt` never matches), returns the deleted count, throws `RangeError` if `graceMs` is not > 0 (reuse detection needs revoked rows to stay for a while); NOT scoped to a user; no caller yet (runner pending). `rotate`'s `$transaction` uses `ROTATE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS` (`maxWait` and `timeout` 10 s each, constant in the file) because Prisma's 2 s / 5 s defaults made parallel rotations fail on the remote pooler; the other transactions keep the defaults. Does not check expiry, user status or reuse detection (service job). `findByTokenHash` is called by `refreshTokens` and `logout`, `rotate` and `revokeAllByUser` (reuse detection) by `refreshTokens`, (all in `auth.service.js`); `revokeById` has no caller since logout moved to `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx` (kept and tested); `deleteStaleSessions` has no caller (verified by grep: no code, no npm script). Login part (unchanged): `createLoginSessionTx({ userId, tokenHash, expiresAt, now, meta? })`: ONE interactive `prisma.$transaction` — (a) `user.update` sets `lastLoginAt = now` and reads back the user's email, (b) creates `RefreshSession` (stores the token HASH only; `meta.ipAddress`, `meta.userAgent`), (c) creates the `LOGIN_SUCCESS` AuditLog row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)`. All-or-nothing (a missing user → Prisma P2025, duplicate hash → P2002, both roll back everything). Returns `{ sessionId }`. `meta` is `{ ipAddress?, userAgent? }`; omitted fields are stored as NULL. Called by `loginWithPassword` (`startLoginSession`)                            | `createLoginSessionTx(input)`, `findByTokenHash`, `revokeById`, `revokeAllByUser`, `rotate`, `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx`, `deleteStaleSessions`                                                             |
| `src/repositories/password-reset.repository.js` | DB access for `PasswordResetOtp` (only hashes stored; callers pass `otpHash` = bcrypt of the OTP and `resetTokenHash` = `hashToken(token)`). `client` = prisma client or a transaction client. `createOtp(client, { userId, otpHash, expiresAt, ipAddress })` → row (app UUID). `invalidateOpenOtps(client, userId, now)` → `updateMany` sets `invalidatedAt` on rows with `usedAt` and `invalidatedAt` null. `findLatestByUserId(userId)` → newest row or null. `findLatestByEmail(email)` → newest row of the user with that exact email, plus `user: { id, email, status }`, or null (callers pass the normalized lowercase email). `incrementAttempts(id)` → atomic `increment`, returns `{ attempts }`. `invalidateOtp(id, now)` → sets `invalidatedAt`. `markVerifiedTx({ otpId, userId, email, resetTokenHash, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — sets `verifiedAt` + `resetTokenHash` and writes the `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED` audit row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)` (an audit failure rolls both back); returns nothing. `createOtpTx({ userId, email, otpHash, expiresAt, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — invalidate open OTPs, create the new OTP (`meta.ipAddress`), write `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` audit row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)`; returns `{ otpId }`. `resetPasswordTx({ otpId, userId, email, passwordHash, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — FIRST `passwordResetOtp.updateMany({ id, usedAt: null })` sets `usedAt` and throws `OTP_ALREADY_USED` if 0 rows matched (the loser of a race with the same token fails before changing anything), then update `User.passwordHash` + `mustChangePassword = false`, `refreshSession.updateMany` sets `revokedAt = now` where `revokedAt` is null, write `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED` audit row; returns nothing. It does not check the OTP/token (that is the service's job). No expiry/attempt logic here                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `createOtp`, `invalidateOpenOtps`, `findLatestByUserId`, `findLatestByEmail`, `incrementAttempts`, `invalidateOtp`, `markVerifiedTx`, `createOtpTx`, `resetPasswordTx`                                           |

Use cases `listUnavailability` (manager or own coach via `assertCanViewAvailability`), `createUnavailability`, `updateUnavailability`, `deleteUnavailability` (managers only via `assertManager`; coach 404 first, then in ONE `$transaction`: per-coach advisory lock `coach_unavailability_<coachId>`, row lookup by id AND coach, rules, overlap check, write, audit row). Pure rule functions (exported for unit tests): `assertCreateDates(input, today)` (start >= today, end >= start), `resolveUpdate(stored, patch, today)` (merges stored + patch, then: ended leave 409 `UNAVAILABILITY_PAST_IMMUTABLE`; ongoing leave locks `startDate`; a changed date cannot be in the past; end >= start on the merged values), `computeCoverage`, `findAffectedSessions({ coachId, startDate, endDate, now })` (read-only: window from `facilityDateRangeToUtc`, starts at `now` for a range already begun, <= 100 not-cancelled sessions of the coach with class name and all assigned coach ids in one query, their total count, and ONE query for the other coaches' leave; no N+1). "Today" is `getTodayInTimezone(FACILITY_TIMEZONE)`. Writes nothing outside `coach_unavailability` and `audit_logs`: no session, booking or appointment is touched. `generateSessionsForWindow`, the cron job and the `isCoachOnLeave` seam are NOT touched. |

Never password hash, role or user status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `toCoachResponse`, `toPublicCoachResponse`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `src/repositories/coachAvailability.repository.js` | SLAPP 66/111 S3. `findWeekByCoachId(coachId, client)` (days + slots ordered by start, `select` only), `lockCoachAvailability(coachId, client)` (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('coach_availability_' + coachId))`, same pattern as the class lock), `replaceWeek(coachId, days, client, now)` (deleteMany days, createMany days, createMany slots with app UUIDs and one shared timestamp; reuses `parseTimeTo1970Date` from `schedule.repository`). Touches only `coach_availability_days` and `coach_availability_slots`. |

Dates are `@db.Date`, written as midnight UTC. |
| `src/repositories/coachAvailability.mapper.js` | SLAPP 66/111 S3. `toWeeklyAvailabilityResponse({ coachId, rows, timezone })`: always 7 days in order 1..7 (a weekday with no row = `{ isAvailable: false, slots: [] }`), `isConfigured` = any saved row, `updatedAt` = latest row timestamp as ISO UTC or explicit `null`, times via `formatTimeHHMM` from `schedule.mapper`. |
| `src/repositories/coachUnavailability.mapper.js` | SLAPP 66/111 S4. `toDateString`, `toUnavailabilityResponse` (`id, coachId, startDate, endDate` as `YYYY-MM-DD`, `reason`, `note` explicit `null`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` ISO UTC), `toAffectedSessionResponse(session, coverage)` (`sessionId, classId, title` = class name, `startAt`, `endAt` ISO UTC, `coverage`), `toUnavailabilityWithImpact` (leave + `affectedSessions` + `affectedTotal`, flat). |

### Membership Plans feature (`src/constants`, `src/validators`, `src/repositories`, `prisma/seeds`)

| Path                                            | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Exports                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/validators/membershipPlan.validation.js`   | Zod schema `membershipPlanSchema` for membership plan entities/rows. Cross-field rules: `creditType === 'TIME'` requires `creditsIncluded === 0` and `creditRefill === null`; non-time plans require `creditsIncluded >= 1`; `creditRefill === 'MONTHLY'` allowed only with `billingCycle === 'MONTH'`; recurring cycles require `kind === 'SUBSCRIPTION'`; trims strings and coerces price string/number to decimal string. Extended for SLAPP 84/106: validates `term` (optional string max 100), `recurring` (boolean, default true), `periodMonths` (optional int 1/3/6/12), `joiningFee` (number >= 0, default 0). `validateCreatePlan`: strips UI helper fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc`; enforces mutual exclusivity between `membershipId` (UUID) and `membership: { name, description }` (both together -> 400). SL-MP-03: `listPlansQuerySchema` and `validateListPlansQuery`. SL-MP-04/05 & SLAPP 84/106: `validateUpdatePlanBody` (rejects non-editable fields incl. `joiningFee`, allows `membershipId` UUID or null to reassign/detach, rejects nested `membership` object) and `validatePlanStatusBody`. **2026-11-08:** list query uses `paginationQueryFields` (`limit`, was `pageSize`); the eight message literals (price max, `periodMonths`, `membershipId`, credits/refill rules, mutual exclusivity) are `Messages.*`. Also exports `createPlanSchema` (built on `membershipPlanSchema`, handles the 3 creation modes) and `planStatusBodySchema`. | `{ membershipPlanSchema }`, `createPlanSchema`, `planStatusBodySchema`                          |
| `src/validators/pagination.validation.js`       | The one paging contract of every list (query `?page=1&limit=20`): `pageField` (coerced int 1..`MAX_PAGE`, default 1, `PAGE_INVALID`), `limitField` (coerced int 1..`MAX_LIMIT` = 100, default 20, `LIMIT_INVALID` "Limit must be a whole number between 1 and 100") and `paginationQueryFields = { page, limit }`, spread into the plan, class, session, coach, cafe item, inventory and order query schemas. `buildSizeField` and every `pageSize` field were removed 2026-11-08; an old `pageSize` query param is simply ignored (strict schemas reject it as unknown).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `pageField`, `limitField`, `paginationQueryFields`                                              |

### Scheduling feature (`src/constants`, `src/validators`, `src/repositories`, `src/services`, `src/controllers`, `src/routes`) — BE-SCH-1, BE-SCH-2, BE-SCH-3

Recurring slots under `/api/v1/classes/:id/slots` (routes in `class.routes.js`) and sessions under `/api/v1/sessions`. Behaviour, conflict rules and the daily job are described in §22; this table lists the files (added 2026-11-06, the files existed but had no rows here).

| Path                                      | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Exports                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/constants/session.js`                | Session source, rolling window, capacity limits and override field lists (see the `src/constants/` table).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | All session constants                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `src/validators/schedule.validation.js`   | Zod schemas and 400 middlewares for slots and sessions: create slots (`startDate`, `days[].times[]` with `startTime`, `endTime`, `coachIds`, `capacity`), update slot, list slots query, list sessions query (`from`/`to` required), create one-off session, patch session (with `overrideReasons`), cancel session (reason required). **2026-11-08:** every message is a `Messages.*` constant; the list query uses the shared `paginationQueryFields` (`page`, `limit`, default 20, was `pageSize` default 100); validation failures are 400.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `createSlotsBodySchema`, `updateSlotBodySchema`, `listSlotsQuerySchema`, `listSessionsQuerySchema`, `createSessionBodySchema`, `patchSessionBodySchema`, `cancelSessionBodySchema`, `validateListSlotsQuery`, `validateCreateSlotsBody`, `validateUpdateSlotBody`, `validateListSessionsQuery`, `validateCreateSessionBody`, `validatePatchSessionBody`, `validateCancelSessionBody`                                                                |
| `src/validators/coachAvailability.validation.js` | SLAPP 66/111 S2 (no route uses it yet). Strict Zod schemas (unknown keys -> 400 "Unknown field", reusing `Messages.ORDER_BODY_UNKNOWN_FIELD`) and `validateBody` / `validateParams` middlewares. **Weekly hours** `saveWeeklyAvailabilityBodySchema`: `{ days: [{ dayOfWeek 1-7, isAvailable boolean, slots: [{ startTime, endTime }] }] }`, **exactly 7 entries, `dayOfWeek` 1-7 each once, any order** (the PUT replaces the WHOLE week, so a missing, duplicate or extra day is a 400: a wrong count is reported on `days`, a repeated weekday on `days.N.dayOfWeek`, and the absent weekdays on `days` as "days is missing dayOfWeek 4, 5"), at most 10 slots a day, `HH:mm` 24h, `endTime` later than `startTime` (no midnight crossing), no overlapping slots in a day (touching slots allowed; the message is `AVAILABILITY_SLOTS_OVERLAP_DETAIL` and names the day and both slots, and the service re-checks it), an available day needs >= 1 slot, an unavailable day must have none; output slots are sorted by start time. **Leave** `createUnavailabilityBodySchema`: `{ startDate, endDate, reason, note? }` (real calendar dates via `isCalendarDate` from `cafeOrder.validation`, `endDate` >= `startDate`, reason trimmed 1-200, note trimmed <= 1000; note is `null` when omitted, null or blank). `updateUnavailabilityBodySchema`: any subset, at least one field, returns only the sent fields, a null or blank note clears it, date order checked only when both dates are sent (a single date is compared with the stored row by the service). `unavailabilityParamsSchema` (`coachId` and `id` UUIDs). Exports the schemas plus `validateSaveWeeklyAvailabilityBody`, `validateCreateUnavailabilityBody`, `validateUpdateUnavailabilityBody`, `validateUnavailabilityParams`. The "no past dates" rule is NOT here (needs the facility clock): service, S4. | SLAPP 66/111 S4: the leave validators are now used by the routes. Added `listUnavailabilityQuerySchema` / `validateListUnavailabilityQuery` (strict: `from`, `to` as calendar dates, `from <= to`, shared `page` / `limit`; unknown params 400). The params schema key was renamed from `id` to `unavailabilityId` to match the route (S2 test updated).
| `src/repositories/schedule.repository.js` | Prisma access for slots and sessions: date/time parsing helpers, per-class advisory lock (`pg_advisory_xact_lock`), slot lookup / conflict / create / update / soft deactivate, generated future sessions per slot, session lookup / list / create / update, coach overlap check, session coach replacement, coach details.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `parseTimeTo1970Date`, `parseDateOnly`, `acquireClassAdvisoryLock`, `findSlotById`, `findSlotsByClassId`, `findActiveSlotConflict`, `createSlotWithCoaches`, `updateSlot`, `softDeactivateSlot`, `findGeneratedFutureSessionsForSlot`, `deleteSessionsByIds`, `findSessionById`, `findSessions`, `findSessionAtStartTime`, `findCoachOverlappingSessions`, `replaceSessionCoaches`, `createSession`, `updateSession`, `findCoachesByIdsWithDetails` |
