| `src/services/schedule.service.js`        | Scheduling logic: facility timezone helpers (`localDateTimeToUtc`, `getTodayInTimezone`, `addDaysToDateStr`, `getIsoDayOfWeek`; the date helpers are also reused by `cafeOrder.service`), 21-day rolling generation (`generateSessionsForWindow`), slot change reconciliation, slot CRUD and session list/get/create/patch/cancel/restore, run inside `prisma.$transaction` with the class advisory lock. **2026-11-08:** `assertClassReadableByUser(classRecord, user, notFound)` reports a class the caller may not see as 404 (`CLASS_NOT_FOUND` for slots, `SESSION_NOT_FOUND` for sessions) instead of 403, and `getSession` answers 404 `SESSION_NOT_FOUND` for a private session asked by a MEMBER; real authorization failures (a COACH editing or cancelling someone else's session) stay 403. `listSessions` returns `pagination` via `toSkipTake` / `buildPagination`. **Known limitation (unchanged):** for MEMBER and COACH the role filter runs after the page is cut, so `pagination.total` counts the visible sessions of the returned page only. | `localDateTimeToUtc`, `getTodayInTimezone`, `addDaysToDateStr`, `getIsoDayOfWeek`, `generateSessionsForWindow`, `reconcileSlotChange`, `listSlots`, `createSlots`, `updateSlot`, `deleteSlot`, `listSessions`, `getSession`, `createSession`, `patchSession`, `cancelSession`, `restoreSession`                                                                                                                                                     |

### Cafe Orders feature (`src/constants`, `src/validators`, `src/repositories`, `src/services`, `src/controllers`, `src/routes`) — SLAPP-79/82 phases 1 and 2

Order queue reads (phase 1) and the one-step status change (phase 2, `PATCH /orders/:orderId/status`, OWNER/ADMIN). Orders are created by a later ticket (there is no create endpoint). `paymentStatus` is always `not_tracked`; no payment data is stored, no endpoint can change it, and no summary returns revenue.

**Validation note (strict vs. older cafe validators):** the cafe order validators in `src/validators/cafeOrder.validation.js` are **strict**: any unknown query parameter is rejected with 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`, one `errors[]` entry per key (`{ field: <key>, message: "Unknown query parameter" }`). The phase 2 status body is strict too: any key besides `status` is 400 (`{ field: <key>, message: "Unknown field" }`). The older cafe validators in `src/validators/cafe.validation.js` (categories, items, inventory) **strip/ignore** unknown keys. This is deliberate: it stops parameters such as `source`, `userId` or `createdBy` from being silently accepted on an endpoint with per-user visibility. Do not "align" either side without a decision.

| Path                                       | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Exports                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `src/constants/cafeOrder.js`               | Status / payment-status enums and maps, read/admin role lists, search limit (see the `src/constants/` table).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | All order constants                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `src/validators/cafeOrder.validation.js`   | Zod schemas built with `z.strictObject` (unknown keys -> 400, expanded to one error per key). `listOrdersQuerySchema`: `from`, `to` (real calendar dates `YYYY-MM-DD`, `from` <= `to`), `status` (lowercase enum), `search` (trimmed, max 100), `page` (shared `pageField`), `limit` (1-100, default 20); a repeated parameter (array) is 400. `orderSummaryQuerySchema`: `from`, `to` only. `orderDetailQuerySchema`: no keys at all. `orderIdParamSchema`: UUID. Phase 2: `updateOrderStatusBodySchema` (strict, required lowercase `status`; unknown keys "Unknown field"; built with the shared `strictObjectWith` helper behind `strictQuery` / `strictBody`), stored on `req.validatedBody`. Middlewares store results on `req.validatedQuery` / `req.validatedParams` and answer via `sendValidationFailed` (400). **2026-11-08:** `page` / `limit` come from `paginationQueryFields`; strict-query failures are 400 (were 422).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `isCalendarDate`, `listOrdersQuerySchema`, `orderSummaryQuerySchema`, `orderDetailQuerySchema`, `orderIdParamSchema`, `validateListOrdersQuery`, `validateOrderSummaryQuery`, `validateOrderIdParam`, `validateOrderDetailQuery`, `updateOrderStatusBodySchema`, `validateUpdateOrderStatusBody` |
| `src/services/cafeOrder.access.js`         | The only place the visibility rule lives. `buildOrderScope(user)` returns `{}` for OWNER/ADMIN and `{ createdById: user.id }` for every other role (unknown roles included); throws without an authenticated user id. `user` is always `req.user` from `authenticate` (DB role), never request input.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `isOrderAdmin`, `buildOrderScope`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `src/repositories/cafeOrder.repository.js` | Data access. `buildOrderWhere(scope, { createdAt, status, search })` always wraps the scope in `AND` so no filter can overwrite or OR around it; search is escaped (`escapeLikePattern`) and case-insensitive over `orderNumber`, `customerName` and `items.some.itemName`. `listOrders` (newest first: `createdAt` DESC, `id` DESC; selects creator id/fullName and `_count.items`), `countOrders`, `findOrderById` (`findFirst` with the scope; items ordered by `itemName`, `id`), `getOrderSummary` (two counts plus `cafeOrderItem.groupBy(itemName)` summing quantity, ordered quantity DESC then `itemName` ASC). Phase 2: `findOrderStatusById` (id, orderNumber, status) and `updateOrderStatusWithAudit({ orderId, from, to, audit }, client)`: one `$transaction` with `updateMany({ where: { id, status: from }, data: { status: to } })` (only status; `updatedAt` automatic), then `createAuditLog(tx, audit)` and the detail re-read; returns `null` when the conditional write matched nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `buildOrderWhere`, `listOrders`, `countOrders`, `findOrderById`, `getOrderSummary`, `findOrderStatusById`, `updateOrderStatusWithAudit`                                                                                                                                                          |
| `src/repositories/cafeOrder.mapper.js`     | Lowercase `status` / `paymentStatus`; `Decimal` money to numbers via `cafe.mapper.decimalToNumber` (`totalAed`, `unitPriceAed`); `lineTotalAed` computed with `Prisma.Decimal` (exact, e.g. 3 x 0.10 = 0.3); `customerName` null when empty; `createdBy { id, fullName }`; `itemCount`; ISO timestamps.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `toCafeOrderListResponse`, `toCafeOrderItemResponse`, `toCafeOrderDetailResponse`                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `src/services/cafeOrder.service.js`        | `toCreatedAtRange({ from, to }, timeZone)` turns inclusive facility-local dates (`env.FACILITY_TIMEZONE`, default Asia/Dubai) into a UTC `{ gte, lt }` range, reusing `localDateTimeToUtc` / `addDaysToDateStr` from `schedule.service`. `listOrders({ user, query })` (scope + filters, DB paging, `buildPagination`), `getOrder({ user, orderId })` (404 `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` for missing and out-of-scope alike), `getSummary({ user, query })` (range defaults to today via `getTodayInTimezone`; a single bound defaults the other to today, or to the same day if that would invert the range; returns `range`, `orderCount`, `preparingCount` (PREPARING only), `topSeller` (first ranked row or null), `itemSalesHistory`). Phase 2: `getAllowedTransitions(from)` / `isAllowedTransition(from, to)` over `ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS`; `updateOrderStatus({ actor, orderId, status, meta })` re-checks `isOrderAdmin` (403), 404 `ORDER_NOT_FOUND`, 409 `INVALID_ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITION` with `details { from, to, allowed }`, writes with audit (`CAFE_ORDER_STATUS_CHANGED`, `actorId`/`actorRole`, `userId` null, `reason` `"<orderNumber> (<id>)"`, `details { orderId, orderNumber, from, to }`, request ip / user agent); on a lost race re-reads: gone 404, changed 409 from the current status. Returns the detail view. | `toCreatedAtRange`, `listOrders`, `getOrder`, `getSummary`, `getAllowedTransitions`, `isAllowedTransition`, `updateOrderStatus`                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `src/controllers/cafeOrder.controller.js`  | Thin handlers passing `req.user` and validated input: `listCafeOrders` (200 `{ data, pagination }`), `getCafeOrder` (200 `{ data }`), `getCafeOrderSummary` (200 `{ data }`), phase 2 `patchCafeOrderStatus` (200 `{ message: ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED_SUCCESS, data }`, passes `req.user` as actor and `getRequestMeta(req)`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `listCafeOrders`, `getCafeOrder`, `getCafeOrderSummary`, `patchCafeOrderStatus`                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `tests/helpers/cafeOrder.helper.js`        | `createTestCafeOrder(prefix, { createdById, items, ...overrides })` seeds an order plus line items directly (total computed with `Prisma.Decimal`; order number starts with `prefix`); `deleteTestCafeOrders(prefix)` (items cascade).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `createTestCafeOrder`, `deleteTestCafeOrders`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

**Client upload flow (Kotlin/Retrofit/OkHttp):** (1) `POST /api/v1/uploads/profile-image/token` with JSON `{ "contentType": "image/png" }` → `data.upload` (`method`, `url`, `headers`) + `data.expiresAt`; (2) the client sends `PUT` to `data.upload.url` with exactly `data.upload.headers` and the raw image bytes as the body (OkHttp `RequestBody` of the same mimetype; at most `data.maxSizeBytes`, before `expiresAt`) straight to Vercel Blob; the JSON response of that PUT contains `url`; (3) `POST /api/v1/auth/register` as plain JSON including `"profileImageUrl": "<that url>"`. The upload request format (URL, header names, API version 12) was taken from the installed `@vercel/blob` 2.8.0 SDK source and has NOT been exercised against the real API (**Unverified** against the live service).

Supports `actorId` and `actorRole` directly (meta has `ipAddress`/`userAgent`). `recordLoginFailure({ userId?, email, reason, meta? })` writes a `LOGIN_FAILED` row (`reason` from `LoginFailureReason`, attempted email, ip, user agent; `userId` omitted for unknown emails). Uses `AuditAction` from `constants/audit.js`. `recordLoginFailure` is called by `loginWithPassword` (through `recordFailureSafely`) SL-MP-04/05: `createAuditLog` also accepts optional `details` (JSON, stored in `AuditLog.details`; old and new values of a change). | `createAuditLog`, `recordLoginFailure` |

### `tests/`

Integration tests hit the real DB from `.env` (remote Neon, ~300ms/query, ~1.6s per interactive transaction) via supertest + `createApp()`, or call repositories directly. DB-backed suites set `jest.setTimeout(30000)` in-file (jest's 5s default is too short) and clean up their own users/audit rows. There is no empty placeholder test file any more (`auth.me.test.js` was filled in the Ticket 6 work). Per-suite counts in the Status column were carried over from earlier revisions of this file; a "—" means the count was **not re-verified** in this re-sync (the totals above come from the real run).

## 4. Prisma Models & Enums

**Enums:** `Role` (MEMBER, COACH, ADMIN, OWNER) · `AccountStatus` (PENDING, ACTIVE, DISABLED) · `Gender` (MALE, FEMALE, NON_BINARY, PREFER_NOT_TO_SAY) · `Relationship` (PARENT, SPOUSE, SIBLING, CHILD, FRIEND, GUARDIAN, OTHER) · `AuditAction` (LOGIN_SUCCESS, LOGIN_FAILED, STAFF_CREATED, STAFF_CREATE_DENIED, PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED, PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED, PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_FAILED, PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED, LOGOUT, COACH_UPDATED, COACH_STATUS_CHANGED, PLAN_CREATED, PLAN_UPDATED, PLAN_STATUS_CHANGED, CAFE_ORDER_STATUS_CHANGED, COACH_AVAILABILITY_UPDATED, COACH_UNAVAILABILITY_CREATED, COACH_UNAVAILABILITY_UPDATED, COACH_UNAVAILABILITY_DELETED, SESSION_COACH_REPLACED) · `CoachType` (SALARIED, FREELANCE) · `CoachStatus` (ACTIVE, INACTIVE) · `LoginFailureReason` (INVALID_CREDENTIALS, ACCOUNT_PENDING, ACCOUNT_DISABLED — still in the schema but no longer a column type: `AuditLog.reason` is now plain text) · `PlanKind` (SUBSCRIPTION, CREDIT_PACK, SINGLE_VISIT, TRIAL) · `PlanBillingCycle` (MONTH, QUARTER, HALF_YEAR, YEAR, ONE_OFF) · `PlanCreditType` (CLASS, APPOINTMENT, FACILITY, TIME) · `PlanCreditRefill` (MONTHLY, ONCE) · `PlanValidityUnit` (DAY, WEEK, MONTH) · `PlanStartsOn` (PURCHASE_DATE, FIRST_BOOKING) · `PlanVisibility` (PUBLIC, HIDDEN) · `PlanStatus` (ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ARCHIVED) · `ClassVisibility` (PUBLIC, PRIVATE) · `ClassStatus` (ACTIVE, DEACTIVATED, ARCHIVED) · `ClassType` (ONSITE, ONLINE) · `SessionState` (OPEN, FULL, CANCELLED) · `CafeItemAvailability` (AVAILABLE, SOLD_OUT) · `CafeItemVisibility` (PUBLIC, STAFF_ONLY) · `CafeOrderStatus` (PREPARING, READY, SERVED) · `CafeOrderPaymentStatus` (NOT_TRACKED)

Generator `prisma-client-js`; datasource `postgresql` with `url = env("DATABASE_URL")` and `directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")` (non-pooled URL Prisma uses for migrations; declared optional in `env.js`, documented in `.env.example`). 22 models (verified 2026-11-11; the 3 new ones are `CoachAvailabilityDay`, `CoachAvailabilitySlot`, `CoachUnavailability`, documented after `Coach` below).

**Model `User`** — used by: `user.repository`, `staff.repository`, `session.repository` (`lastLoginAt`), `prisma/seed-owner.js` (via `createUser`)

| Field                     | Type               | Constraints                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `id`                      | String             | `@id` (UUID set by app, no DB default)                                         |
| `fullName`                | String             | —                                                                              |
| `email`                   | String             | `@unique`                                                                      |
| `phone`                   | String             | not unique (unique index dropped)                                              |
| `whatsappNumber`          | String?            | optional                                                                       |
| `gender`                  | Gender             | —                                                                              |
| `birthDay`                | Int?               | optional                                                                       |
| `birthMonth`              | Int?               | optional                                                                       |
| `profileImageUrl`         | String?            | optional; Vercel Blob URL                                                      |
| `passwordHash`            | String             | —                                                                              |
| `role`                    | Role               | `@default(MEMBER)`                                                             |
| `status`                  | AccountStatus      | `@default(PENDING)`                                                            |
| `mustChangePassword`      | Boolean            | `@default(false)`; true for staff-created users                                |
| `waiverAcceptedAt`        | DateTime?          | optional                                                                       |
| `registrationCompletedAt` | DateTime?          | optional                                                                       |
| `lastLoginAt`             | DateTime?          | set on successful login                                                        |
| `emergencyContact`        | EmergencyContact?  | 1:1 relation                                                                   |
| `coach`                   | Coach?             | 1:1 relation (set for COACH users created through `POST /admin/staff/coaches`) |
| `refreshSessions`         | RefreshSession[]   | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `auditLogs`               | AuditLog[]         | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `passwordResetOtps`       | PasswordResetOtp[] | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `cafeOrders`              | CafeOrder[]        | 1:n relation (SLAPP-79/82; orders RESTRICT user deletion)                      |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime           | `@default(now())`                                                              |
| `updatedAt`               | DateTime           | `@updatedAt`                                                                   |

**Model `EmergencyContact`** — used by: `user.repository.completeRegistrationTx`

| Field          | Type          | Constraints                                 |
| -------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `id`           | String        | `@id` (UUID set by app)                     |
| `userId`       | String        | `@unique`, FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`) |
| `name`         | String?       | optional                                    |
| `phone`        | String        | —                                           |
| `relationship` | Relationship? | optional                                    |
| `createdAt`    | DateTime      | `@default(now())`                           |
| `updatedAt`    | DateTime      | `@updatedAt`                                |

**Model `Coach`** — coach profile of a login account. Only coach-specific data; name, email, phone and image stay on `User`. Used by: `coach.repository` (and `staff.service.createCoach` through it)

| Field       | Type        | Constraints                                         |
| ----------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`        | String      | `@id` (UUID set by app); the `coachId` of the API   |
| `userId`    | String      | `@unique`, FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`)         |
| `coachType` | CoachType   | `@@index`                                           |
| `bio`       | String?     | optional, max 1000 enforced by validation           |
| `status`    | CoachStatus | `@default(ACTIVE)`, `@@index`                       |
| `createdAt` | DateTime    | `@default(now())`                                   |
| `updatedAt` | DateTime    | `@updatedAt` (also set explicitly on coach updates) |
| `availabilityDays` / `availabilitySlots` / `unavailability` | `CoachAvailabilityDay[]` / `CoachAvailabilitySlot[]` / `CoachUnavailability[]` | relations added by SLAPP 66/111 S1 (all cascade on coach delete); also the `classes`, `slots`, `sessions` relations of the class models |

**Model `CoachAvailabilityDay`** (`coach_availability_days`, SLAPP 66/111 S1) — one row per coach and ISO weekday (Monday = 1). No code uses it yet (repository is S3).

| Field         | Type                      | Constraints                                                                        |
| ------------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `coachId`     | String                    | part of `@@id([coachId, dayOfWeek])`, FK to Coach (`onDelete: Cascade`)            |
| `dayOfWeek`   | Int                       | part of the id, CHECK 1-7                                                          |
| `isAvailable` | Boolean                   | `@default(true)`; false or a missing row = coach NOT assignable that weekday       |
| `slots`       | CoachAvailabilitySlot[]   | 1:n, cascade                                                                       |
| `createdAt` / `updatedAt` | DateTime      | `@default(now())` / `@updatedAt`                                                   |

**Model `CoachAvailabilitySlot`** (`coach_availability_slots`, S1) — one time range of a day. Facility wall-clock `HH:mm` (`FACILITY_TIMEZONE`), no midnight crossing.

| Field                     | Type     | Constraints                                                                                          |
| ------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String   | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                                              |
| `coachId`, `dayOfWeek`    | String, Int | composite FK to `CoachAvailabilityDay` (cascade); `coachId` also FK to Coach (cascade); CHECK day 1-7 |
| `startTime`, `endTime`    | DateTime | `@db.Time(0)`; CHECK `endTime > startTime`; `@@unique([coachId, dayOfWeek, startTime])`              |
| `createdAt` / `updatedAt` | DateTime | `@default(now())` / `@updatedAt`                                                                     |

**Model `CoachUnavailability`** (`coach_unavailability`, S1) — date-specific unavailability (leave); inclusive facility-local dates. Used since S4 by `coachUnavailability.repository.js` (CRUD, overlap check, list); the S1 indexes `(coachId, startDate, endDate)` and `(startDate, endDate)` serve the overlap check, the list and the other-coaches leave lookup.

| Field                     | Type     | Constraints                                                                     |
| ------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String   | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                         |
| `coachId`                 | String   | FK to Coach (cascade); `@@index([coachId, startDate, endDate])`, `@@index([startDate, endDate])` |
| `startDate`, `endDate`    | DateTime | `@db.Date`; CHECK `endDate >= startDate`                                        |
| `reason`                  | String   | free text, CHECK not blank and <= 200                                           |
| `note`                    | String?  | optional, CHECK <= 1000                                                         |
| `createdAt` / `updatedAt` | DateTime | `@default(now())` / `@updatedAt`                                                |

**Model `RefreshSession`** — opaque refresh tokens; only the SHA-256 hash is stored. Used by: `session.repository.createLoginSessionTx`. The repository now has `findByTokenHash`, `revokeById`, `revokeAllByUser`, `rotate` (refresh ticket part 1/5), `refreshTokens` (service) uses them via `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh`; `logout` (service) revokes sessions through `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx` via `POST /api/v1/auth/logout` (authenticated, scoped to the caller, audited); password reset revokes all active sessions in its own transaction. No `replacedBy` column (decided: not needed; reuse detection = revoked token presented → `revokeAllByUser` + 401, to be built in the service).

| Field                     | Type      | Constraints                                 |
| ------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String    | `@id` (UUID set by app)                     |
| `userId`                  | String    | FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`), `@@index` |
| `tokenHash`               | String    | `@unique`                                   |
| `expiresAt`               | DateTime  | —                                           |
| `revokedAt`               | DateTime? | optional                                    |
| `ipAddress` / `userAgent` | String?   | optional                                    |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime  | `@default(now())`                           |

**Model `PasswordResetOtp`** — one row per password-reset OTP request. Used by: `password-reset.repository.js` and `password-reset.service.js` (forgot-password, verify-otp, reset-password). Only hashes are stored (bcrypt OTP hash, SHA-256 reset-token hash).

| Field            | Type      | Constraints                                                      |
| ---------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`             | String    | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                          |
| `userId`         | String    | FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`), `@@index([userId, createdAt])` |
| `otpHash`        | String    | bcrypt hash of the 4-digit OTP                                   |
| `expiresAt`      | DateTime  | OTP expiry (now + `OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES`)                          |
| `attempts`       | Int       | `@default(0)`; wrong verifications, capped by `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` |
| `verifiedAt`     | DateTime? | set when the OTP is verified                                     |
| `usedAt`         | DateTime? | set when the password reset completes (no reuse)                 |
| `invalidatedAt`  | DateTime? | set when superseded by a newer OTP or attempts are exhausted     |
| `resetTokenHash` | String?   | `@unique`; SHA-256 of the token returned by verify               |
| `ipAddress`      | String?   | optional                                                         |
| `createdAt`      | DateTime  | `@default(now())`                                                |

**Model `AuditLog`** — login success/failure & staff creation audit trail. Used by: `audit-log.repository` (`createAuditLog`, `recordLoginFailure`), `session.repository`, `staff.service` SL-MP-04/05: nullable `details Json` holds the old and new values of a plan change (`PLAN_UPDATED`: `{ changes: { field: { from, to } }, version: { from, to } }`; `PLAN_STATUS_CHANGED`: `{ from, to, reason }`); `AuditAction` gained `PLAN_UPDATED` and `PLAN_STATUS_CHANGED`. The audit table has no entity-id column, so a plan's entries are found through `reason` = `"<name> (<id>)"`.

| Field                     | Type        | Constraints                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String      | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                                                             |
| `userId`                  | String?     | FK to User (`onDelete: SetNull`); null for unknown email                                                            |
| `action`                  | AuditAction | —                                                                                                                   |
| `reason`                  | String?     | set on failures (LoginFailureReason values or dynamic staff denial reason from Messages.STAFF_CREATE_DENIED_REASON) |
| `email`                   | String?     | attempted email (normalized)                                                                                        |
| `actorId`                 | String?     | caller user id (for staff creation / admin actions)                                                                 |
| `actorRole`               | String?     | caller role (for staff creation / admin actions)                                                                    |
| `details`                 | Json?       | optional; old/new values of plan changes (SL-MP-04/05)                                                              |
| `ipAddress` / `userAgent` | String?     | optional                                                                                                            |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime    | `@default(now())`; `@@index([userId, createdAt])`                                                                   |

**Model `Membership`** (`@@map("memberships")`) — groups membership plans. Used by: `membership.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.mapper.js`. Raw SQL unique index on `LOWER(name)` (`memberships_name_lower_idx`).

| Field         | Type             | Constraints                                                            |
| ------------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`          | String           | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                |
| `name`        | String           | max 120 chars; unique index on `LOWER(name)`                           |
| `description` | String?          | optional, max 1000 chars                                               |
| `createdAt`   | DateTime         | `@default(now())`                                                      |
| `plans`       | MembershipPlan[] | 1-to-many relation to `MembershipPlan` (onDelete Restrict on the plan) |

**Model `MembershipPlan`** (`@@map("membership_plans")`) — membership plans catalog seeded from Glofox. Used by: `membershipPlan.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.mapper.js`, `prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js`. Note: virtual `section` (`'online_coaching'` when `creditType === 'TIME'`, else `'class_membership'`) is derived at runtime in the mapper/repository and is NEVER stored in the database. Indexes: `status`, `kind`, `creditType`, composite `(status, creditType)` (SL-MP-03), `membershipId` (SLAPP 84/106), unique `externalRef`, partial unique `LOWER(name) WHERE status != 'ARCHIVED'`. The database collation is `C.UTF-8` (byte order, case-sensitive), so name ordering uses `LOWER("name")` in raw SQL (see §17). Note: `activeClientCount` is still a stub seam returning 0 (no assignments table exists yet in the database).

| Field                      | Type              | Constraints                                                                           |
| -------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                       | String            | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                               |
| `name`                     | String            | max 120 chars; partial unique index on `LOWER(name) WHERE status != 'ARCHIVED'`       |
| `description`              | String?           | optional, max 1000 chars                                                              |
| `membershipId`             | String?           | FK to `Membership` (`onDelete: Restrict`), indexed (`@@index([membershipId])`)        |
| `term`                     | String?           | optional, max 100 chars (e.g. "Monthly", "Annual", "12-Week")                         |
| `recurring`                | Boolean           | `@default(false)`                                                                     |
| `periodMonths`             | Int?              | 1, 3, 6, 12 or null                                                                   |
| `joiningFee`               | Decimal(10, 2)    | `@default(0)`; DB check `>= 0 AND <= 100000`                                          |
| `kind`                     | PlanKind          | SUBSCRIPTION, CREDIT_PACK, SINGLE_VISIT, TRIAL                                        |
| `billingCycle`             | PlanBillingCycle  | MONTH, QUARTER, HALF_YEAR, YEAR, ONE_OFF                                              |
| `creditType`               | PlanCreditType    | CLASS, APPOINTMENT, FACILITY, TIME                                                    |
| `creditsIncluded`          | Int               | no default; DB check `>= 0`; 0 for TIME plans, >= 1 for non-time plans                |
| `creditRefill`             | PlanCreditRefill? | optional; MONTHLY, ONCE; null for TIME plans; MONTHLY only with billingCycle MONTH    |
| `validityValue`            | Int               | DB check `>= 1`                                                                       |
| `validityUnit`             | PlanValidityUnit  | DAY, WEEK, MONTH                                                                      |
| `price`                    | Decimal(10, 2)    | DB check `>= 0 AND <= 100000`                                                         |
| `startsOn`                 | PlanStartsOn      | PURCHASE_DATE, FIRST_BOOKING                                                          |
| `oneTimePurchasePerClient` | Boolean           | `@default(false)`                                                                     |
| `visibility`               | PlanVisibility    | required, no default; PUBLIC, HIDDEN                                                  |
| `status`                   | PlanStatus        | `@default(ACTIVE)`; ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ARCHIVED                                        |
| `externalRef`              | String?           | `@unique`; Glofox plan code used for idempotent upsert                                |
| `version`                  | Int               | `@default(1)`; bumped automatically via DB trigger ONLY when commercial fields change |
| `createdAt`                | DateTime          | `@default(now())`                                                                     |
| `updatedAt`                | DateTime          | `@updatedAt`                                                                          |

**Models and enums added by SL-CLS-01** (all `@@map` to snake_case plural tables; ids are app-generated UUID strings). Enums `ClassVisibility` (PUBLIC, PRIVATE), `SessionState` (OPEN, FULL, CANCELLED).

- `Class` -> `classes`: `name`, `description?`, `durationMin` (CHECK > 0), `baseCapacity` (CHECK >= 1), `intensity` (CHECK 1-5, NOT NULL; seeded records placeholder 3), `classType` (enum ClassType ONSITE, ONLINE; default ONSITE), `classPassEnabled` (Boolean default false), `visibility`, `classpassSpots` (default 0, CHECK: 0 when classPassEnabled=false, 0..baseCapacity when true), `colour?` (CHECK `#RRGGBB`), `photoUrl?`, `status` (enum ClassStatus ACTIVE, DEACTIVATED, ARCHIVED; default ACTIVE; dropped legacy active column), `externalRef?` (unique), `version` (default 1, bumped by trigger), timestamps. Partial unique index on `LOWER(name) WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'`; index on `status`.
- `ClassCoach` -> `class_coaches` (PK classId + coachId; both FKs CASCADE). `ClassEligiblePlan` -> `class_eligible_plans` (PK classId + planId; class CASCADE, plan RESTRICT).
- `ClassSlot` -> `class_slots`: `classId` (CASCADE), `dayOfWeek` (ISO 1-7), `startTime` (`TIME(0)`, Asia/Dubai wall clock), `capacity?` (override, NULL = base capacity), `active`, timestamps; unique `(classId, dayOfWeek, startTime)`. `ClassSlotCoach` -> `class_slot_coaches` (both FKs CASCADE).
- `ClassSession` -> `class_sessions`: `classId` (RESTRICT), `slotId?` (SET NULL; null = one-off), `startAt`, `endAt` (CHECK endAt > startAt), `capacity` (CHECK >= 1, snapshot), `state` (default OPEN), `isPrivate` (default false), `cancelReason?` (CHECK only when CANCELLED), `overriddenFields` (text[] default []), `version` (trigger), timestamps; unique `(classId, startAt)`; indexes on `startAt`, `slotId`. `ClassSessionCoach` -> `class_session_coaches` (session CASCADE, coach RESTRICT).
- `Coach` gained relations `classes`, `slots`, `sessions`; `MembershipPlan` gained `eligibleClasses` (Prisma-level only).
- Added later by BE-SCH-1/2/3 (migration `20261027130000_add_scheduling_fields`, see §22): `Class.timezone` (default `Asia/Dubai`); `ClassSlot.startDate` (`DATE`) and `ClassSlot.endTime` (`TIME(0)`), the unconditional unique `(classId, dayOfWeek, startTime)` replaced by the partial unique index `class_slots_active_unique_idx` `WHERE active = true` (raw SQL, not in the Prisma schema), plus `@@index([classId])`; `ClassSession.source` (default `GENERATED`), snapshots `durationMin?`, `intensity?`, `classType?` (text), `facility?`, `description?`, `image?`, and `overrideDetails` (JSON, default `{}`), plus indexes `(startAt, classId)` and `(slotId, startAt)`. Known drift: the DB has `class_slots.startDate DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE` from the migration but the Prisma schema declares no default (`prisma migrate diff` reports it).

**Models and enums added by SLAPP-79/80 (Cafe Catalogue)** (mapped to `cafe_categories` and `cafe_items`; ids are app-generated UUID strings). Enums `CafeItemAvailability` (AVAILABLE, SOLD_OUT) and `CafeItemVisibility` (PUBLIC, STAFF_ONLY).

- `CafeCategory` -> `cafe_categories`: `id` (UUID PK), `name` (unique case-insensitive index on `LOWER(name)`), `description?`, timestamps. Has 1:n relation to `CafeItem`.
- `CafeItem` -> `cafe_items`: `id` (UUID PK), `name`, `categoryId` (FK to `cafe_categories`, `onDelete: Restrict`), `description?`, `nutrition?` (free-form JSON object; the API validates only that it is an object with string keys, no fixed fields), `priceAed` (`Decimal(10, 2)`, CHECK > 0), `imageUrl?`, `availability` (`CafeItemAvailability`, default AVAILABLE), `visibility` (`CafeItemVisibility`, default PUBLIC), `isTracked` (Boolean, default true), `stockQuantity?` (Int, CHECK >= 0), `unitCost?` (`Decimal(10, 2)`, CHECK >= 0), `externalRef?` (unique String, Glofox variant ID for idempotent seed), timestamps. Indexes on `categoryId`, `visibility`, `availability`, and `(visibility, availability)`. DB CHECK `cafe_items_tracked_consistency_check` ensures tracked items have non-null stock and unit cost and untracked items have both NULL. There is NO DB constraint tying stock = 0 to `SOLD_OUT`: the rule is service-layer logic in `cafeItem.service` (auto `SOLD_OUT` when tracked stock reaches 0 on create/update; `available` with stock 0 → 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`) and, since SLAPP-79/81 sub-task 2, `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` (quantity and SOLD_OUT in one write), exposed as `PATCH /api/v1/cafe/items/:itemId/stock` since sub-task 3. Restocking never flips `SOLD_OUT` back to `AVAILABLE` automatically. Inventory uses these existing fields without a migration.

**Models and enums added by SLAPP-79/82 (Cafe order queue, phase 1)** (mapped to `cafe_orders` and `cafe_order_items`; ids are app-generated UUID strings; migration `20261106120000_add_cafe_orders`). Enums `CafeOrderStatus` (PREPARING, READY, SERVED; API lowercase) and `CafeOrderPaymentStatus` (NOT_TRACKED only; API `not_tracked`). Used by: `cafeOrder.repository.js`, `cafeOrder.mapper.js`, `tests/helpers/cafeOrder.helper.js`.

- `CafeOrder` -> `cafe_orders`: `id` (UUID PK), `orderNumber` (unique, CHECK not blank), `status` (`CafeOrderStatus`, default PREPARING), `createdById` (NOT NULL, FK to `User`, `onDelete: Restrict`), `customerName?` (free-text snapshot, not a FK; searched by the list), `totalAed` (`Decimal(10, 2)`, CHECK >= 0; placed order total, not paid revenue), `paymentStatus` (`CafeOrderPaymentStatus`, default NOT_TRACKED), timestamps. Indexes `(createdAt, id)`, `(createdById, createdAt)`, `status`. 1:n `items`.
- `CafeOrderItem` -> `cafe_order_items`: `id` (UUID PK), `orderId` (FK to `cafe_orders`, `onDelete: Cascade`), `itemName` (snapshot, CHECK not blank), `unitPriceAed` (`Decimal(10, 2)` snapshot, CHECK >= 0), `quantity` (Int, CHECK > 0). No timestamps and **no FK to `cafe_items`**, so catalogue renames, price changes and deletes never alter history. Trigger `cafe_order_items_snapshot_immutable` (raw SQL in the migration) rejects any UPDATE of `orderId`, `itemName`, `unitPriceAed`, `quantity`. Indexes `orderId`, `itemName`.
- `User` gained `cafeOrders` (Prisma-level relation).
- Phase 2 (migration `20261106130000_add_cafe_order_status_audit_action`): `AuditAction` gained `CAFE_ORDER_STATUS_CHANGED`. Status changes write only `cafe_orders.status` (and `updatedAt`); the order item trigger and NOT_TRACKED-only payment enum are unchanged.

---

## 5. API Endpoints

Auth column: "—" public; "Bearer + role" = `authenticate` then `authorize`. Standard bodies: success `{ success: true, message, data }` (lists add a top-level `pagination`, reads have no `message`); error `{ success: false, message, code, errors? }` (`code` on every error since 2026-10-13). Lists: every list endpoint takes `page` / `limit` and returns `pagination` (`GET /api/v1/plans` also a top-level `counts`); the former `meta` / `pageSize` exception was removed 2026-11-08.

| Method           | Path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Auth                              | Rate limit                                   | Handler                                                    | Status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET              | `/health`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | —                                 | —                                            | `health.routes.js`                                         | ✅ done — 200 liveness (liveness; deliberately NOT rate limited; wrong method -> 405 `Allow: GET`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| GET              | `/health/ready`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | —                                 | `publicLimiter` (per IP; it runs a DB query) | `health.routes.js`                                         | ✅ done — 200 / 503 DB readiness                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| GET              | `/api-docs/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | —                                 | `publicLimiter`                              | `docs.routes.js` (Swagger UI)                              | ✅ done — 200 HTML, public                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

## 6. Environment Variables

| Name                                 | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `NODE_ENV`                           | `development` \| `test` \| `production`; default `development`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PORT`                               | HTTP port; default `5000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LOG_LEVEL`                          | pino level (`fatal`…`trace`, `silent`); default `info`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CORS_ORIGINS`                       | Comma-separated browser origins or `*`; default `*`. Entries are trimmed, lowercased, empties dropped, and must be bare origins (`http(s)://host[:port]`, no path/query/trailing slash) or startup fails. **In production `*`, unset or an empty list makes the app refuse to start**; `*` stays allowed in development/test. Mobile apps send no `Origin` and are unaffected. `.env.example` documents a placeholder production list (domain not final) |
| `DATABASE_URL`                       | PostgreSQL connection string; required (pooled URL on Neon)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DIRECT_URL`                         | Optional string in `env.js`; used by `schema.prisma` as `directUrl` (non-pooled connection Prisma uses for migrations). Listed in `.env.example`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FACILITY_TIMEZONE`                  | String; default `Asia/Dubai`. Facility wall-clock zone for class/slot scheduling, the daily generation job and the cafe order `from`/`to` date ranges                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BCRYPT_SALT_ROUNDS`                 | Integer; default `12`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET`                  | **Required**; min 32 chars and must not look like a placeholder (`CHANGE_ME`, `replace_me`, `your_secret`, `random secret`, `placeholder`, `secret_here`, case-insensitive, with `_`, `-` or space between words); the app will not start otherwise. `.env.example` ships `CHANGE_ME_...` on purpose, so a copied template fails until replaced                                                                                                          |
| `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`              | Positive whole number + unit `s`, `m`, `h` or `d` (`/^[1-9]\d*[smhd]$/`); default `15m`. Bare numbers (`900` = 900 ms in jsonwebtoken), `0m`, empty, decimals, uppercase and other units are rejected at startup with a clear error                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`             | Integer; default `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`               | Window in ms; default `900000` (15m)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`                     | Max requests per window; default `1000` (was `10` until 2026-10-10; in test env the limiter uses 100000 unless this is set)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `TRUST_PROXY`                        | `true`/`false`/number/string → Express `trust proxy`; default `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SMTP_HOST`                          | SMTP server hostname (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SMTP_PORT`                          | SMTP server port integer (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SMTP_SECURE`                        | `true`/`false` boolean; default `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SMTP_USER`                          | SMTP auth username (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SMTP_PASS`                          | SMTP auth password (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SMTP_FROM`                          | Sender email address (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `REFRESH_SESSION_CLEANUP_GRACE_DAYS` | Integer > 0; default `7`. Grace before expired/revoked refresh sessions may be deleted by `deleteStaleSessions` (the runner is not built yet; must stay > 0 for reuse detection)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`       | Window in ms for `/auth/refresh` + `/auth/logout`; default `900000` (15m)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX`             | Optional int > 0; max requests per window per IP for refresh + logout. Unset → 60 (100000 when `NODE_ENV=test`); an explicit value is honoured in every environment. Keep it unset/commented in the `.env` used for tests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES`                 | Integer; default `10`. OTP lifetime for password reset (read by `env.js` since 2026-10-11; also in local `.env`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS`                   | Integer; default `5`. Max wrong OTP attempts per OTP (not in local `.env`, default applies)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`              | Vercel Blob token; optional string, no default (profile image upload token signing; missing → 503 on the token endpoint); server-only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEED_OWNER_EMAIL`                   | **Seed only** (`npm run prisma:seed`), NOT read by `env.js`: owner email, signup email rules (example `owner@example.com`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEED_OWNER_PASSWORD`                | **Seed only**: owner password, `PASSWORD_REGEX` + max 72, never printed; `.env.example` placeholder is deliberately invalid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SEED_OWNER_FULL_NAME`               | **Seed only**: owner full name, 2–100 chars (example `First Owner`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SEED_OWNER_PHONE`                   | **Seed only**: owner phone, E.164-style (example `+12025550100`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SEED_MEMBERSHIP_FILE`               | **Seed only**: optional path to the Glofox `.xlsx` for `npm run seed:membership-plans` (CLI argument wins)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEED_CLASSES_FILE`                  | **Seed only**: optional path to the `.xlsx` for `npm run seed:classes` (CLI argument wins)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEED_CAFE_FILE`                     | **Seed only**: optional path to the `.xlsx` for `npm run seed:cafe` (CLI argument wins)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

**Confirmed stale in the local `.env` on 2026-10-11 (names only, read by no code; `.env` not edited, owner asked to remove them):** `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`, `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN`, `JWT_RESET_SECRET`, `JWT_RESET_EXPIRES_IN`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `COOKIE_SECURE`. The local `.env` does not set `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, `TRUST_PROXY`, `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS` or `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` (defaults apply).

**Earlier list — present in local `.env` but NOT read by `env.js` (legacy, unused):** `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN` (refresh lifetime is `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`), `JWT_RESET_SECRET`, `JWT_RESET_EXPIRES_IN`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `COOKIE_SECURE`. (`SMTP_*` moved out of this list: they are read now. Which of the others still exist in the local `.env` is **Unverified**; `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_*` and `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_*` were confirmed present earlier this session.)
