### Image Upload Token (`POST /api/v1/cafe/uploads/image/token`)

- Uses client-token direct upload pattern via `@vercel/blob` helper `createImageUploadToken(contentType, 'cafe-items')`.
- Accepts `{ contentType: 'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp' }` (unsupported type -> 400 envelope code `VALIDATION_ERROR` with the field message "Image must be a JPEG, PNG or WebP file" in `errors[]`; `IMAGE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED` is not an envelope code on this route).
- Returns identical envelope shape to profile image token: `{ clientToken, expiresAt, maxSizeBytes: 4194304, allowedContentTypes, upload: { method: 'PUT', url, headers } }`.

### Glofox Store Seed (`prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js`)

- **Command:** `npm run seed:cafe` (defined in `package.json`).
- **Flags:**
  - `--dry-run`: parses sheet and calculates all planned categories/items without database writes.
  - `--force`: overwrites existing items by `externalRef` (default skips existing items, preserving manual admin adjustments to stock, price, availability, and visibility).
- **Source:** Sheet `"Store"` of `docs/Spirit_Level_Glofox_Business_Data.xlsx`.
- **Seed Notes:**
  - `unitCost` is set to `0.00` placeholder (unit purchase cost was not tracked in Glofox export).
  - Seeded stock quantities reflect inventory counts as of extraction on 6 Oct 2026.
  - STYRKR comma-separated category mapped cleanly to `"Supplements"`.
  - Whitespace normalized (trimmed and collapsed repeated whitespace).
- **Seeded Stats:** 61 items, 12 categories, 18 untracked (Coffee, Protein Shakes, Protein Coolers), 43 tracked (23 sold out, 20 available), 7 staff_only items, 61 image URLs present (all https).

### Data Model & Migration

- Migration `20261102130000_add_cafe_catalogue` + reversible `down.sql`:
  - `cafe_categories`: `id` (UUID PK), `name` (unique case-insensitive index on `LOWER(name)`), `description`, timestamps.
  - `cafe_items`: `id` (UUID PK), `externalRef` (unique, nullable, Glofox variant ID), `name`, `categoryId` (FK to `cafe_categories`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT`), `description`, `nutrition` (JSONB), `priceAed` (`DECIMAL(10,2)`, CHECK > 0), `imageUrl`, `availability` (`CafeItemAvailability`, default `AVAILABLE`), `visibility` (`CafeItemVisibility`, default `PUBLIC`), `isTracked` (BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE), `stockQuantity` (INT, nullable, CHECK NULL or >= 0), `unitCost` (`DECIMAL(10,2)`, nullable, CHECK NULL or >= 0), timestamps.
  - CHECK constraints (4): `cafe_items_price_aed_check`, `cafe_items_stock_quantity_check`, `cafe_items_unit_cost_check`, `cafe_items_tracked_consistency_check` (`isTracked = false` ⇒ `stockQuantity` and `unitCost` both NULL; `isTracked = true` ⇒ both NOT NULL). No other CHECK (no stock-0 ⇒ sold-out constraint).
  - Indexes: `cafe_items_categoryId_idx`, `cafe_items_visibility_idx`, `cafe_items_availability_idx`, `cafe_items_visibility_availability_idx`.
  - `down.sql` drops the FK, both tables and both enums.

### Cafe error codes (`src/constants/errors.js`, inventory foundations added 2026-11-03)

Envelope: `{ success: false, message, code, errors? }`. The `AppErrors` key and the wire `code` differ only for `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND_BODY` (wire code is `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`).

| Code (wire)                      | HTTP | Thrown when                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------- | ---- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`             | 404  | `PATCH` / `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` with an unknown id (`AppErrors.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND` (BODY)      | 400  | `POST /items` or `PATCH /items/:itemId` whose body `categoryId` does not exist (`AppErrors.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND_BODY`, same wire code as the 404)                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CATEGORY_EXISTS`                | 409  | `POST` / `PATCH` category with a name another category already has (case-insensitive; also on the DB unique-index race)                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS`             | 409  | `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` while any item (any visibility) references the category                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`                 | 404  | `GET`, `PATCH`, `PATCH .../availability`, `PATCH .../visibility` with an unknown id; also `GET /items/:itemId` by a MEMBER for a `staff_only` item                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED`               | 409  | SLAPP-79/81 `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` rejects an existing item with isTracked false; returned by `PATCH /items/:itemId/stock` since sub-task 3 (nothing is written)                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`       | 400  | `POST /items` with `availability: available` and tracked stock 0; `PATCH .../availability` to `available` on a tracked item with stock 0                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ITEM_TRACKED_STOCK_REQUIRED`    | 400  | `POST /items` tracked without `stockQuantity` / `unitCost`; `PATCH` switching untracked -> tracked without both; `PATCH` setting a tracked item's stock or cost to `null`                                                                                                                                                         |
| `IMAGE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED`         | 400  | `createImageUploadToken` (storage layer) given an unsupported type. On `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` the validator rejects first with 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (field message)                                                                                                                                                   |
| `IMAGE_UPLOAD_NOT_CONFIGURED`    | 503  | `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` while Blob storage is not configured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `IMAGE_UPLOAD_TOKEN_FAILED`      | 502  | `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` when signing the client token fails. `blob.storage.js` throws `AppErrors.IMAGE_UPLOAD_TOKEN_FAILED` (renamed from the unused `IMAGE_UPLOAD_FAILED` on 2026-11-08; the wire code is unchanged). `IMAGE_UPLOAD_NOT_CONFIGURED` (503) and `IMAGE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED` (400) are `AppErrors` too        |
| `VALIDATION_ERROR`               | 400  | Any cafe body / query / path validation failure via `sendValidationFailed` (all failing fields in `errors[]`). Includes the field message `ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE` text when `PATCH /items/:itemId` receives `availability` or `visibility`. Note `AppErrors.VALIDATION_ERROR` itself is 400; cafe routes always answer 400 |
| `UNAUTHORIZED` / `TOKEN_EXPIRED` | 401  | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FORBIDDEN`                      | 403  | Role not allowed (COACH / MEMBER on writes; roles are checked before validation)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`              | 429  | `cafeReadLimiter` (reads) or `cafeLimiter` (writes / upload token) exhausted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`             | 405  | Any other method on a cafe route, with `Allow` set per route (see the audit table above)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

`ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE` is a `Messages` key (field-level message in `errors[]`), not an `AppErrors` entry and not an envelope `code`.

### OpenAPI (`docs/openapi.yaml`, tag `Cafe`)

- All 11 catalogue operations are documented (categories 4, items 6, upload token 1), plus SLAPP-79/81 `PATCH /items/{itemId}/stock` (`updateCafeItemStock`, with `CafeStockStatus`, `UpdateCafeItemStockRequest`, `CafeItemStockUpdate`, `CafeItemNotTracked`) and `GET /inventory` (`listCafeInventory`, with `CafeInventoryItem`, `CafeMethodNotAllowedGetOnly`) and `GET /inventory/summary` (`getCafeInventorySummary`, with `CafeInventorySummary`), plus a representative 405 operation per route (documented under `put` / `delete`) carrying the correct `Allow` header. `DELETE /items/:itemId` appears only as a 405.
- New components: parameters `CafeCategoryId` / `CafeItemId`; headers `CafeCategoryLocation` / `CafeItemLocation`; responses `ValidationFailed` (it was referenced by the Classes section but never defined), `CafeCategoryNotFound`, `CafeCategoryExists`, `CafeItemNotFound`, `CafeStockRuleViolation`, `CafeItemValidationFailed`, `CafeMethodNotAllowed*` (Collection, Category, Item, PatchOnly, PostOnly); schemas `CafeCategory`, `CafeItem`, `CafeItemCategory`, `Create/UpdateCafeCategoryRequest`, `Create/UpdateCafeItemRequest`, `CafeItemAvailability(Request)`, `CafeItemVisibility(Request)`. The upload token endpoint reuses `ProfileImageTokenRequest` / `ProfileImageUploadToken`.
- Known pre-existing gaps (not touched): the Classes section references `#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse`, `#/components/responses/NotFound` and `#/components/responses/Conflict`, which are not defined, and the `Classes` tag is not declared in `tags`.

## 25. Slot upsert fix, eligibility 405 and full-run status (2026-11-03)

Found on the SLAPP-80 branch (which contains the BE-SCH scheduling commit `12bb2917`) and fixed without any schema change or migration:

- **`class.repository.upsertSlot`** used the compound key `classId_dayOfWeek_startTime`, which no longer exists after migration `20261027130000_add_scheduling_fields` replaced the full unique index with the partial `class_slots_active_unique_idx` (`WHERE active = true`) and added NOT NULL `startDate` / `endTime`. It now uses `findFirst({ classId, dayOfWeek, startTime, active: true })`, updates by id, else creates with `startDate` (today at the facility timezone) and `endTime` (`startTime` + `durationMin`). `prisma/seeds/classes.seed.js` passes `durationMin`. Behaviour notes: an inactive slot at the same class/day/time is ignored (a new slot is created); a `startTime + durationMin` past midnight would wrap `endTime` (no seeded slot does: 29 slots, latest start 20:00, none crossing midnight).
- **`src/routes/class.routes.js`**: added `router.all('/:id/eligibility', methodNotAllowed(['PUT']))` (GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE answer 405 with `Allow: PUT`; before, they fell through to 404).
- **`tests/unit/schedule.adapters.mock.test.js`**: the two slot-POST tests used a hard-coded `startDate` of 2026-11-01, which became "in the past"; they now use today + 7 days.
- **Verification:** `jest class.seed class.methods schedule.adapters` 3 suites / 23 tests pass; `jest class schedule` 25 suites / 289 tests pass (623 s); `eslint .` clean; full run 140 suites / 2057 tests, 2055 pass.
- **Open item:** `tests/integration/plan.list.test.js` (2 tests, "lists the full catalogue (60+ plans) within 300 ms plus the network", sort=name and sort=-price) fails on a latency budget against the remote Neon DB (e.g. 1033 ms vs a 910 ms budget); it is a network-timing check, unrelated to these changes, and also failed in the earlier partial run.
- **DB state after the run:** `cafe_items` 61, `cafe_categories` 12; no `test_` rows in cafe tables, classes or membership_plans; no users with `slcls` in the email.

## 26. API contract hardening — rate limiters, one list shape, one status standard (2026-11-08)

Scope (three approved tasks, nothing else): (1) a rate limiter on every route, (2) one list response shape, (3) status codes and messages that match the situation. Decisions approved before coding: validation is **400 everywhere**; list shape is `pagination { page, limit, total, totalPages }` with `counts` at the top level; `publicLimiter` on `/health/ready`, `/api-docs*`, the 404 fallback and the 405 handlers, `GET /health` left unlimited; breaking changes allowed because no client exists yet.

### Rate limiters

| Route group                                             | Limiter                                     | Placement                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| `POST /admin/staff`, `POST /admin/staff/coaches`        | `staffLimiter`                              | after `authenticate` + `authorize` |
| `GET /coaches`, `/coaches/me`, `/coaches/:id`           | `coachReadLimiter`                          | after `authorize`                  |
| `PATCH /coaches/:id`, `/coaches/:id/status`             | `coachLimiter`                              | after `authorize`                  |
| `GET /auth/me`, `PATCH /uploads/profile-image`          | `profileLimiter`                            | after `authenticate`               |
| `/health/ready`, `/api-docs/*`, 404 fallback, every 405 | `publicLimiter` (one shared per-IP counter) | first                              |
| `GET /health`                                           | none (liveness probe)                       | —                                  |

All use the existing factories (`createPlanLimiter`, `createAuthLimiter`) with the default 1000 per 15 minutes; no existing limit changed. `methodNotAllowed()` now returns `[publicLimiter, handler]`, so no route file needed a second import.

### List shape (endpoints changed)

`GET /plans` (`meta` -> `pagination` + top-level `counts`, `pageSize` -> `limit`), `GET /classes` (`meta` -> `pagination`, `pageSize` -> `limit`, default 100 -> 20), `GET /sessions` (same). Already conforming and only re-pointed to the shared constants / `paginationQueryFields`: `GET /coaches`, `/cafe/items`, `/cafe/inventory`, `/cafe/orders`. Not lists: `/cafe/inventory/summary`, `/cafe/orders/summary`. Unpaged by design: `GET /memberships`, `GET /classes/:id/slots`.

### Status and message changes

| Change                                                                                                              | Where                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Every 422 -> 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (plans, memberships, classes, slots, sessions, cafe, orders, inventory)         | `sendValidationFailed`, `AppErrors` (`CAPACITY_BELOW_BOOKED`, `CANCEL_REASON_REQUIRED`, `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND_BODY`), `class.service`, `class.repository`, `plan.service`, `schedule.service` |
| Malformed `:id` (plans, classes, slots, sessions) 404 -> 400                                                        | `idParam.validation.js`, routed in `plan` / `class` / `session` routes                                                                                                                      |
| Cafe image-token bad MIME 422 -> 400 (matches the profile token)                                                    | `cafe.validation.js`                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Hidden session / class-slot list 403 -> 404                                                                         | `schedule.service` (`assertClassReadableByUser`, `getSession`)                                                                                                                              |
| P2025 race on cafe category / item writes 500 -> 404                                                                | `orNotFound` in `prismaError.util.js`                                                                                                                                                       |
| Unmapped P2002 "User already exists" -> generic 409 `RESOURCE_ALREADY_EXISTS`; register keeps `USER_ALREADY_EXISTS` | `errorHandler`, `auth.service`                                                                                                                                                              |
| Register 409 and 413 now carry a `code`                                                                             | `auth.service`, `errorHandler` (`PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`)                                                                                                                                        |
| "Category ID" for an item id; "positive integer" for limit 101                                                      | `cafe.validation.js`, `messages.js`                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLASS_INACTIVE` text covers archived classes                                                                       | `messages.js`                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Dangling `Location` removed                                                                                         | `membership.controller`, `cafeCategory.controller`                                                                                                                                          |
| Wrong method on auth / staff / coaches / uploads / health 404 -> 405                                                | the five route files                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Missing body: `field: "body"`, "Request body is required" (was `""` and Zod text)                                   | `validate.middleware`, `validationError.util`                                                                                                                                               |
| 404 fallback no longer echoes the URL                                                                               | `notFound.middleware`                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Plan error codes UPPER_SNAKE                                                                                        | `errors.js` (`PLAN_DUPLICATE_NAME`, `PLAN_ARCHIVED`, `PLAN_IN_USE`, `PLAN_INVALID_TRANSITION`)                                                                                              |
| All message literals -> `Messages.*`; unused constants removed; `blob.storage` uses `AppErrors`                     | see §3 `messages.js` / `errors.js` rows                                                                                                                                                     |

### Deviations from the approved plan (all small)

- Plan error codes were planned as `DUPLICATE_NAME`, `PLAN_ARCHIVED`, `PLAN_IN_USE`, `INVALID_TRANSITION`; built as `PLAN_DUPLICATE_NAME`, `PLAN_ARCHIVED`, `PLAN_IN_USE`, `PLAN_INVALID_TRANSITION` so every code equals its `AppErrors` key.
- `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND` was planned to become a real 404; a body reference to a missing membership is a validation problem like a missing class plan or category (400 with an `errors[]` entry), so the unused AppError was removed instead.
- Added beyond the list (same kind of mismatch): 405 for the other routers, `CLASS_INACTIVE` wording, the shared `toFieldErrors`, and a general P2025 -> 404 safety net in `errorHandler`.

### OpenAPI (`docs/openapi.yaml`)

Rewritten with a Node script (kept out of the repo) against the live routers, then verified two ways. Static, permanent: `tests/unit/openapi.spec.test.js`. Live, scratch: every new and changed endpoint was called through supertest on the local DB and the real body validated against the spec with closed object schemas. Result: 66 operations + 49 405 stubs on 52 paths; added `/memberships` GET/POST, `/classes/{id}/slots` GET/POST, `/classes/{id}/slots/{slotId}` PATCH/DELETE, `/sessions` GET/POST, `/sessions/{id}` GET/PATCH, `/sessions/{id}/cancel`, `/sessions/{id}/restore`, and the three `/api-docs` paths; shared components `Page`, `Limit`, `ClassId`, `SlotId`, `SessionId`, `ValidationError` (one), `TooManyRequests` (with `Retry-After` / `RateLimit-*` headers), `PayloadTooLarge`, `RouteNotFound`, `ClassNotFound`, `SlotNotFound`, `SessionNotFound`, `ScheduleConflict`, `ClassInactive`, `CafeItemBadRequest`; schemas `Membership`, `Slot`, `Session`, `CoachBrief`, `PlanSectionCounts`, `PlanMembershipRef` and the request / result shapes; `PlanDetail` / `PlanListItem` / `ClassResponse` gained the fields the API really sends (`creditNote`, `membership`, `membershipId`, `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`, `joiningFee`; `scheduleSummary` object, `eligiblePlans`, `recurringSchedule`, `upcomingInstancesSummary`); `PlanCreditRefill` allows `null`. The info description now states the envelope, list contract, status standard, rate limits, formats and CORS once. One validator artefact remains: `CafeOrderSummary.topSeller` is `nullable` + `allOf`, valid OpenAPI 3.0 but unreadable by the `ajv` v6 used for the scratch check.

### Running project log

- **2026-11-08 — API contract hardening, sub-task 1 (rate limiters).** Added 5 limiters, wired 12 routes + docs + 404 + all 405s, structural coverage test, behaviour test. Baseline 155 suites / 2399 tests passed before any change.
- **2026-11-08 — sub-task 2 (list shape).** One `paginationQueryFields`, `buildPagination` only; plans / classes / sessions switched; tests updated; session list test added; 158 suites / 2432 tests passed.
- **2026-11-08 — sub-task 3 (status codes and messages).** 400 standard, id guards, 404 for hidden resources, `orNotFound`, error handler codes, literals to constants, 405 on every router, body-required message; 160 suites / 2475 tests passed.
- **2026-11-08 — OpenAPI.** Rewritten and verified; `openapi.spec.test.js` added; final full run 161 suites / 2483 tests passed.
- **Open items carried forward:** `GET /sessions` role filter after paging (wrong `pagination.total` for MEMBER / COACH, fix = push the visibility rules into the Prisma `where`); security findings from the 2026-11-07 audit (OTP brute force, `Cache-Control`, `mustChangePassword`, public `/api-docs` in production, register enumeration); ONBOARDING.md is still stale (not in scope here).

## 27. SLAPP 66/111 — Coach Availability, Conflicts, Leave & Replacement Assignment (in progress, started 2026-11-11)

Ticket tasks: (1) recurring weekly availability + conflict validation, (2) effect of weekly-hours changes on the schedule, (3) date-specific unavailability (leave), (4) replacement coach for an affected class + cancellation side effects. Plan approved with changes; build order S1-S12. **Status: S1, S2 and S3 done (S3 approved and committed by the owner); S4 written (its tests are not yet run by the owner).**

### Approved decisions (binding for S2-S12)

- **Strict availability:** a coach is assignable only when the interval fits fully inside ONE saved available slot of that facility-local weekday. No saved hours, a missing day row or `isAvailable = false` means NOT assignable. Conflict types inside the existing 409 `COACH_CONFLICT` shape (`details.conflicts[]`): `AVAILABILITY_NOT_SET` (no hours saved for that day), `OUTSIDE_AVAILABILITY`, `COACH_UNAVAILABLE` (leave), `SESSION_OVERLAP`, `APPOINTMENT_OVERLAP`, `APPOINTMENT_BUFFER`. Test fixtures / helpers must seed availability so existing tests keep passing.
- **Scope of the engine:** manual paths only (create session, PATCH `coachIds`, restore, appointments). `generateSessionsForWindow`, the cron job and the `isCoachOnLeave` stub in `schedule.adapters.js` are NOT touched (a conflict there would silently skip a session).
- **No push, no new dependencies** (`firebase-admin`, `device_tokens`, `push.service.js` dropped). In-app notifications instead (migration 3 = `class_bookings`, `member_credit_transactions`, `notifications`; endpoints `GET /notifications`, `GET /notifications/unread-count`, `PATCH /notifications/:id/read`, `POST /notifications/read-all`, own rows only, any role). Class cancellation after commit: per booked client credit refund (idempotent ledger), in-app notification, email. No notification for coach unavailable or replacement.
- `countFutureBookedSessions` stays a seam returning 0; no public booking-creation endpoint (tests create bookings through a helper).
- **Defaults:** leave reason = free text (max 200, note max 1000); overlapping leave for the same coach rejected; no leave add/edit on past dates; "covered" = at least one assigned coach available; buffers = `bufferBeforeMin + bufferAfterMin`; appointment requires `memberId`; open-slot step = appointment-type duration; a coach can only read their own availability; no midnight-crossing slots; times `HH:mm` 24h facility wall-clock.
- Coach-picker is a new sibling `GET /coaches/eligible`; replacement reuses `PATCH /sessions/:id` (`coachIds` + `overrideReasons`); schedule coverage is a sibling `GET /sessions/coverage`. Existing `GET /coaches` and `GET /sessions` responses are unchanged.

### S1 — Migration 1 and Prisma models (done 2026-11-11)

- **Files changed (4):** `prisma/schema.prisma` (3 models, `Coach` relations, 5 `AuditAction` values; `prisma format` also re-aligned the `Coach` model columns), `prisma/migrations/20261111100000_add_coach_availability/migration.sql` and `down.sql` (new), `src/constants/audit.js` (5 mirror values). No endpoint, no service, no test added in S1.
- **Models and constraints:** see §3 migrations row and §4 (`CoachAvailabilityDay`, `CoachAvailabilitySlot`, `CoachUnavailability`).
- **Verification (local throwaway Postgres 18 on port 5544, all 25 migrations applied with `prisma migrate deploy`, not Neon):** `prisma validate` OK; `prisma migrate diff` against the schema shows no drift from this migration (one pre-existing difference: `class_slots.startDate` still has a DB default that the Prisma schema does not declare, not from S1); each constraint rejected its bad row (slot without a day row, day 8, `endTime = startTime`, duplicate slot start, `endDate < startDate`, blank reason); deleting a day cascaded to its slots and deleting a coach cascaded to its leave; `down.sql` removed the 3 tables and the 5 enum values and the migration row; re-applying restored all 3 tables; Prisma client regenerated and the three delegates exist. Full jest suite NOT run for S1 (no runtime code changed).
- **Not applied to Neon.**

### S2 — Constants, time util and validators (written 2026-11-11)

- **Files (7):** new `src/constants/availability.js`, `src/utils/timeRange.util.js`, `src/validators/coachAvailability.validation.js`, `tests/unit/timeRange.util.test.js`, `tests/unit/coachAvailability.validation.test.js`; changed `src/constants/messages.js` (16 new keys, section "Coach availability and unavailability (SLAPP 66/111)", placed after `SLOT_DAYS_REQUIRED`) and this file. Details in §3 (constants, utils, validators, tests rows).
- **Reuse:** `localDateTimeToUtc` / `addDaysToDateStr` (`timezone.util`), `DAY_OF_WEEK` (`constants/class`), `isCalendarDate` (`cafeOrder.validation`), `validateBody` / `validateParams` (`validate.middleware`), existing messages `SLOT_START_TIME_*`, `SLOT_END_TIME_*`, `SLOT_END_BEFORE_START`, `DAY_OF_WEEK_*`, `START_DATE_*`, `COACH_ID_INVALID`, `ORDER_BODY_UNKNOWN_FIELD` ("Unknown field"). `validateCoachIdParam` (`coach.validation`) is reused as is by the S3 routes. No existing behaviour changed.
- **Deviation:** the util is `timeRange.util.js` (repo naming), not `timeRange.js`.
- **Request contract fixed here (S3 and S4 build on it):** weekly hours `PUT` body `{ days: [{ dayOfWeek, isAvailable, slots: [{ startTime, endTime }] }] }` is a FULL-WEEK replace: exactly 7 entries, `dayOfWeek` 1-7 each exactly once (an unavailable day is sent as `{ dayOfWeek, isAvailable: false, slots: [] }`); S3 can therefore delete all of the coach's day and slot rows and insert the 7 days in one transaction; leave create `{ startDate, endDate, reason, note? }`; leave update is a partial of the same. Validation failures are 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `errors[{ field, message }]` where `field` is the Zod path (for example `days.0.slots.1.startTime`).
- **Change request (2026-11-11, before the first test run):** the weekly-hours validator first accepted 1-7 days; it now requires exactly 7 (`DAYS_PER_WEEK`), one per weekday. `Messages.AVAILABILITY_DAYS_INVALID` was reworded ("days must contain exactly 7 entries, one for each dayOfWeek from 1 (Monday) to 7 (Sunday)") and `Messages.AVAILABILITY_DAYS_MISSING(list)` added (16 new keys in the SLAPP 66/111 block). Tests rebuilt around a `fullWeek()` fixture. Still not run.
- **Fix after the first test run (2026-11-11):** `days = 'x'` returned the count message twice. Cause: the count was checked with Zod's `.length(7)`, and Zod runs a length check even after the type check failed whenever the value has a `length` property (a string does; `undefined`, `null` and `{}` do not). The count is now a `.refine`, which is skipped once the type check has failed, so a non-array `days` gives exactly one issue at `days`. Contract unchanged (wrong type or wrong count = one issue at path `days`). Not re-run yet.
- **Decisions to confirm:** touching slots (09:00-12:00 and 12:00-15:00) are allowed; the S7 engine will merge them (`mergeRanges`) so a class that spans both still fits. An available day with no slot, and slots on an unavailable day, are rejected rather than ignored. Output slots are sorted by start time.
- **Not run:** nothing was executed for S2 except Prettier and ESLint on the touched files (both clean). Jest was not run; the commands are in the S2 hand-off message.

### S3 — Weekly availability API (written 2026-11-11)

- **Endpoints:** `GET` and `PUT /api/v1/coaches/:coachId/availability` (flow in §2, rows in §3 and §5, `docs/openapi.yaml` operations `getCoachWeeklyAvailability` and `saveCoachWeeklyAvailability` plus the 405 stub, schemas `AvailabilitySlot`, `AvailabilityDay`, `SaveWeeklyAvailabilityRequest`, `WeeklyAvailability`; the PUT description carries the "replaces the whole week; exactly 7 entries" note).
- **Files (14):** new `src/repositories/coachAvailability.repository.js`, `src/repositories/coachAvailability.mapper.js`, `src/services/coachAvailability.service.js`, `src/controllers/coachAvailability.controller.js`, `tests/unit/coachAvailability.service.test.js`, `tests/unit/coachAvailability.mapper.test.js`, `tests/integration/coaches.availability.test.js`; changed `src/routes/coach.routes.js`, `src/services/coach.access.js` (additive `assertCanViewAvailability`), `src/validators/coachAvailability.validation.js` (overlap message now names day and slots), `src/constants/availability.js` (`DAY_LABELS`), `src/constants/messages.js` (`COACH_AVAILABILITY_SAVED_SUCCESS`, `AVAILABILITY_SLOTS_OVERLAP_DETAIL`), `docs/openapi.yaml`, this file.
- **Reuse:** `findCoachById` (coach lookup, `COACH_NOT_FOUND`), `isCoachManager` / `assertManager` and the owner check (`user.id === caller.id`) of `coach.access`, `validateCoachIdParam`, `coachLimiter` / `coachReadLimiter`, `methodNotAllowed`, `createAuditLog`, `getRequestMeta`, `sendSuccess`, `createAppError`, `parseTimeTo1970Date` and `formatTimeHHMM` from the schedule repository / mapper, `findOverlappingPair` from `timeRange.util`, the advisory-lock pattern of the class scheduling code. No existing endpoint changed behaviour (`coach.access` only gained an export).
- **Decisions:** a coach reading another coach is 403 (not 404); a coach PUT is 403 at the route, before validation; `isConfigured` is true once any week was saved (even all-unavailable); `updatedAt` is the latest day-row timestamp (all rows of a save share one); the overlap rule runs both in the validator (fast fail with the same message) and in the service (authoritative, never left to the DB); two concurrent PUTs for one coach are serialized by an advisory lock; the response also carries `coachId` and `timezone` (facility zone of the HH:mm times).
- **Not run:** nothing was executed except Prettier, ESLint and a YAML parse of the new `openapi.yaml` block. Commands are in the S3 hand-off message.

### S4 — Coach unavailability (leave) CRUD (written 2026-11-11)

- **Endpoints:** `GET` / `POST /api/v1/coaches/:coachId/unavailability`, `PATCH` / `DELETE /api/v1/coaches/:coachId/unavailability/:unavailabilityId` (rows in §3, §5, §15; `docs/openapi.yaml` operations `listCoachUnavailability`, `createCoachUnavailability`, `updateCoachUnavailability`, `deleteCoachUnavailability` plus a 405 stub per path; schemas `CoachUnavailability`, `AffectedSession`, `CoachUnavailabilityWithImpact`, `CreateCoachUnavailabilityRequest`, `UpdateCoachUnavailabilityRequest`; parameter `UnavailabilityId`; responses `UnavailabilityNotFound`, `UnavailabilityConflict`). No migration (table from S1).

- **Reuse:** `findCoachById`, `assertManager` and `assertCanViewAvailability` (coach.access, unchanged), `validateCoachIdParam`, `coachLimiter` / `coachReadLimiter`, `methodNotAllowed`, `createAuditLog`, `getRequestMeta`, `sendSuccess`, `createAppError`, `buildPagination` / `toSkipTake`, `paginationQueryFields`, `isCalendarDate`, `getTodayInTimezone`, `facilityDateRangeToUtc`, the advisory-lock pattern of S3, and the S2 leave validators. No existing endpoint changed behaviour.
- **Rules decided here (owner asked for a proposal):** (a) create: `startDate` must be today or later in the facility timezone (400, field `startDate`); (b) PATCH checks end >= start on the MERGED dates; (c) a leave that has NOT started: every field can change, a changed date cannot be in the past; (d) an ONGOING leave (`startDate` before today, `endDate` today or later): `startDate` is locked (400), `endDate` may be extended or shortened but not to a day before today, `reason` and `note` may change; (e) an ENDED leave (`endDate` before today) cannot be edited or deleted: 409 `UNAVAILABILITY_PAST_IMMUTABLE` (history); (f) DELETE of an ongoing or future leave is allowed (added by mistake, or the coach is back early); (g) overlap = the two inclusive ranges share at least one date, so ranges that touch on a date overlap and ranges on consecutive days do not; 409 `UNAVAILABILITY_OVERLAP` with the existing range in the message and in `details.conflict`; (h) a leave id of another coach is 404 `UNAVAILABILITY_NOT_FOUND`, never revealed.
- **Affected sessions (read-only):** POST and PATCH answer with the leave plus `affectedSessions` (at most 100, by start time: `sessionId, classId, title, startAt, endAt, coverage`) and `affectedTotal`. Upcoming = not cancelled and not yet started; a session counts in the facility day of its `startAt`. `COVERED` = at least one OTHER coach assigned to the session is not on leave that day; no other coach = `UNCOVERED`. Computed after the transaction commits (it does not need to be atomic). Nothing about a session, booking or appointment is written.
- **Seam:** the `isCoachOnLeave` stub in `schedule.adapters.js` was NOT touched or activated (Risk 1: generation and the cron job stay as they are); wiring leave into the manual session paths is S9.
- **Error shapes for clients:** past or inverted dates and a locked `startDate` are 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `errors[{ field, message }]`; overlap and ended leave are 409 with their own stable codes; the new codes are in `docs/openapi.yaml`.
- **Not run:** nothing was executed except Prettier, ESLint and a YAML parse of `openapi.yaml`. Commands are in the S4 hand-off message.
